```
    43→    zone_map: FileZoneMap | None = _UNSET
    44→    dep_graph: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = _UNSET
    45→    complexity_map: dict[str, float] | None = _UNSET

    53→    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord] | None = _UNSET
    54→
    55→
    56→_LANG_RUNTIME_STATE_FIELDS = frozenset(f.name for f in fields(LangRuntimeState))
    57→_LANG_OVERRIDE_FIELDS = tuple(f.name for f in fields(LangRunOverrides))
    58→_LANG_OVERRIDE_DICT_FIELDS = frozenset(
    59→    {

    61→        "review_cache",

    69→    {
    70→        "large_threshold_override",
    71→        "props_threshold_override",
    72→    }
    73→)
    74→_FORWARDED_CONFIG_ATTRS = frozenset(
    75→    {
    76→        # Explicit delegation surface for LangRun -> LangConfig.
    77→        # New LangConfig fields are intentionally opt-in here.
    78→        "name",
    79→        "extensions",
    80→        "exclusions",
    81→        "default_src",
    82→        "build_dep_graph",
    83→        "entry_patterns",
    84→        "barrel_names",
    85→        "phases",

    87→        "get_area",
    88→        "detect_commands",
    89→        "extract_functions",
    90→        "boundaries",
    91→        "typecheck_cmd",
    92→        "file_finder",
    93→        "large_threshold",
    94→        "complexity_threshold",
    95→        "default_scan_profile",
    96→        "setting_specs",
    97→        "runtime_option_specs",
    98→        "detect_markers",
    99→        "external_test_dirs",
   100→        "test_file_extensions",
   101→        "review_module_patterns_fn",
   102→        "review_api_surface_fn",
   103→        "review_guidance",
   104→        "review_low_value_pattern",
   105→        "holistic_review_dimensions",
   106→        "migration_pattern_pairs",
   107→        "migration_mixed_extensions",
   108→        "zone_rules",
   109→        "integration_depth",
   110→        "detect_lang_security_detailed",
   111→        "detect_private_imports",
   112→        "normalize_settings",
   113→        "normalize_runtime_options",
   114→        "scan_coverage_prerequisites",
   115→        "set_runtime_context",
   116→    }
   117→)
   118→
   119→
   120→# LangRuntimeContract is defined in base.types to avoid an import cycle.
   121→
   122→
   123→@dataclass
   124→class LangRun(LangRunStateAccessors):
   125→    """Runtime facade over an immutable LangConfig."""
   126→
   127→    config: LangConfig
   128→    state: LangRuntimeState = field(default_factory=LangRuntimeState)
   129→
   130→    def __getattr__(self, name: str):
   131→        if name in _FORWARDED_CONFIG_ATTRS:
   132→            return getattr(self.config, name)
   133→        raise AttributeError(
   134→            f"{self.__class__.__name__!s} has no attribute {name!r}; "
   135→            "access runtime state via explicit LangRun properties"
   136→        )
   137→
   138→    def __dir__(self):
   139→        """Expose LangConfig + mutable runtime fields for discoverability."""
   140→        return (
   141→            list(super().__dir__())
   142→            + sorted(_FORWARDED_CONFIG_ATTRS)
   143→            + sorted(_LANG_RUNTIME_STATE_FIELDS)
   144→        )
   145→
   146→
   147→def _coerce_lang_override(field_name: str, value: object) -> object:
   148→    """Normalize override values to LangRuntimeState-compatible payloads."""
   149→    if field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_DICT_FIELDS:
   150→        return value or {}
   151→    if field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_LIST_FIELDS:
   152→        return value or []
   153→    if field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_INT_FIELDS:
   154→        return int(value or 0)
   155→    if field_name == "review_max_age_days":
   156→        if value is None:
   157→            return None
   158→        return int(value)
   159→    return value
   160→
   161→
   162→def _apply_lang_overrides(runtime: LangRun, overrides: LangRunOverrides) -> None:
   163→    """Apply override bundle to runtime state via one normalized loop."""
   164→    for field_name in _LANG_OVERRIDE_FIELDS:
   165→        value = getattr(overrides, field_name)
   166→        if value is _UNSET:
   167→            continue
   168→        coerced = _coerce_lang_override(field_name, value)
   169→        if field_name == "review_max_age_days" and coerced is None:
   170→            continue
   171→        setattr(runtime, field_name, coerced)
   172→
   173→
   174→def make_lang_run(
   175→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun,
   176→    overrides: LangRunOverrides | None = None,
   177→) -> LangRun:
   178→    """Build a fresh LangRun for a command invocation."""
   179→
   180→    if isinstance(lang, LangRun):
   181→        runtime = lang
   182→    else:
   183→        runtime = LangRun(config=lang)
   184→        runtime.state.runtime_settings = copy.deepcopy(
   185→            getattr(lang, "_default_runtime_settings", {})
   186→        )
   187→        runtime.state.runtime_options = copy.deepcopy(
   188→            getattr(lang, "_default_runtime_options", {})
   189→        )
   190→
   191→    resolved = overrides if overrides is not None else LangRunOverrides()
   192→    _apply_lang_overrides(runtime, resolved)
   193→    return runtime
   194→
   195→
   196→__all__ = [
   197→    "LangRuntimeContract",
   198→    "LangRun",
   199→    "LangRunOverrides",
   200→    "LangRuntimeState",
   201→    "make_lang_run",
   202→]
   203→
```

```
    38→
    39→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    40→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    41→
    42→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    43→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    44→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    45→    """
    46→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    47→    synthetic = [item for item in order if any(item.startswith(p) for p in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)]
    48→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    49→        return False
    50→    for item in synthetic:
    51→        order.remove(item)
    52→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    53→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    54→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    55→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    56→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    57→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    58→    count = len(synthetic)
    59→    if count:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(
    62→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    63→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    64→                "yellow",
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→    return True
    68→
    69→
    70→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    71→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    72→    return bool(
    73→        plan.get("queue_order")
    74→        or plan.get("overrides")
    75→        or plan.get("clusters")
    76→        or plan.get("skipped")
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    81→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    82→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    83→        return False
    84→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    85→    if recon.resurfaced:
    86→        print(
    87→            colorize(
    88→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    89→                "cyan",
    90→            )
    91→        )
    92→    return bool(recon.changes)
    93→
    94→

   114→

   134→
   135→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   136→    plan: dict[str, object],
   137→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   138→    *,
   139→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   140→    policy=None,

   151→
   152→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   153→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   154→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   155→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   156→        return False
   157→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   158→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   159→        return False
   160→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   161→    if scores.strict is None:
   162→        return False
   163→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   164→        "strict": scores.strict,
   165→        "overall": scores.overall,
   166→        "objective": scores.objective,
   167→        "verified": scores.verified,
   168→    }
   169→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   170→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   171→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   172→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   173→    return True
   174→
   175→
   176→def _has_objective_cycle(
   177→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   178→    plan: dict[str, object],
   179→) -> bool | None:
   180→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   183→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   184→        )
   185→
   186→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   187→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   188→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   189→        return None
   190→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   191→
   192→
   193→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   194→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   195→) -> bool:
   196→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   197→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   198→        return False
   199→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   200→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   201→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   202→        return False
   203→
   204→    try:
   205→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   206→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   207→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   208→            score_display_mode,
   209→        )
   210→
   211→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   212→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   214→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   215→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   216→        return False
   217→    if not queue_empty:
   218→        return False
   219→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   220→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   221→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   222→    # in the next cycle.
   223→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   224→    return True
   225→
   226→
   227→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   228→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   229→    plan: dict[str, object],
   230→) -> bool:
   231→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   232→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   233→        return False
   234→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   235→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   236→        return False
   237→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   238→        plan,
   239→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   240→    )
   241→
   242→
   243→def _subjective_policy_context(
   244→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   245→    plan: dict[str, object],
   246→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   247→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   248→
   249→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   250→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   251→        runtime.state,
   252→        target_strict=target_strict,
   253→        plan=plan,
   254→    )
   255→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   256→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   257→
   258→
   259→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   260→    plan: dict[str, object],
   261→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   262→) -> bool:
   263→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   264→    if changed:
   265→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   266→    return changed
   267→
   268→
   269→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   270→    plan: dict[str, object],
   271→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   272→    *,
   273→    policy,
   274→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   275→) -> bool:
   276→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   277→        plan,
   278→        state,
   279→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   280→            p,
   281→            s,
   282→            policy=policy,
   283→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   284→        ),
   285→    )
   286→    if changed:
   287→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   288→    return changed
   289→
   290→
   291→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   292→    plan: dict[str, object],
   293→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   294→    *,
   295→    target_strict: float,
   296→    policy,
   297→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   298→) -> bool:
   299→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   300→        plan,
   301→        state,
   302→        target_strict=target_strict,
   303→        policy=policy,
   304→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   305→    )
   306→    if changed:
   307→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   308→    return changed
   309→
   310→
   311→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   312→    plan: dict[str, object],
   313→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   314→    *,
   315→    policy=None,
   316→) -> bool:
   317→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   318→    if triage_sync.deferred:
   319→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   320→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   321→            print(
   322→                colorize(
   323→                    "  Plan: review issues changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   324→                    "dim",
   325→                )
   326→            )
   327→        return False
   328→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   329→        return False
   330→    if triage_sync.injected:
   331→        print(
   332→            colorize(
   333→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   334→                "cyan",
   335→            )
   336→        )
   337→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   338→    return True
   339→
   340→
   341→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   342→    plan: dict[str, object],
   343→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   344→    *,
   345→    policy,
   346→) -> bool:
   347→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   348→    current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   349→        strict=snapshot.strict,
   350→        overall=snapshot.overall,
   351→        objective=snapshot.objective,
   352→        verified=snapshot.verified,
   353→    )
   354→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   355→        plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores,
   356→    )
   357→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   358→        return False
   359→    append_log_entry(
   360→        plan,
   361→        "sync_communicate_score",
   362→        actor="system",
   363→        detail={"injected": True},
   364→    )
   365→    return True
   366→
   367→
   368→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   369→    plan: dict[str, object],
   370→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   371→    *,
   372→    policy,
   373→) -> bool:
   374→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   375→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   376→        return False
   377→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   378→        print(
   379→            colorize(
   380→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   381→                "cyan",
   382→            )
   383→        )
   384→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   385→    return True
   386→
   387→
   388→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   389→    plan: dict[str, object],
   390→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   391→) -> bool:
   392→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   393→    if seeded:
   394→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   395→        return True
   396→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   397→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   398→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   399→    if cleared:
   400→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   401→    return cleared
   402→
   403→
   404→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   405→    plan: dict[str, object],
   406→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   407→) -> bool:
   408→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   409→    if changed:
   410→        append_log_entry(
   411→            plan,
   412→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   413→            actor="system",
   414→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   415→        )
   416→    return changed
   417→
   418→
   419→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   420→    *,
   421→    plan: dict[str, object],
   422→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   423→) -> bool:
   424→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   425→    dirty = False
   426→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   427→        dirty = True
   428→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, state):
   429→        dirty = True
   430→    return dirty
   431→
   432→
   433→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   434→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   435→
   436→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   437→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   438→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   439→
   440→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   441→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   442→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   443→    """
   444→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   445→        return False
   446→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   447→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   448→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   449→
   450→
   451→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   452→    *,
   453→    plan: dict[str, object],
   454→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   455→    target_strict: float,
   456→    policy,
   457→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   458→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   459→) -> bool:
   460→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   461→
   462→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   463→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   464→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   465→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   466→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   467→    """
   468→    dirty = False
   469→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   470→
   471→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   472→        plan,
   473→        state,
   474→        policy=policy,
   475→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   476→    ):
   477→        dirty = True
   478→    if not mid_cycle:
   479→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   480→            plan,
   481→            state,
   482→            target_strict=target_strict,
   483→            policy=policy,
   484→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   485→        ):
   486→            dirty = True
   487→    else:
   488→        print(
   489→            colorize(
   490→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   491→                "preserve queue state.",
   492→                "dim",
   493→            )
   494→        )
   495→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   496→        dirty = True
   497→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   498→        dirty = True
   499→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   500→        dirty = True
   501→    if not force_rescan:
   502→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   503→            dirty = True
   504→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   505→            dirty = True
   506→    return dirty
   507→
   508→
   509→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
   510→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   511→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   512→    try:
   513→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   514→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   515→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   516→        return
   517→
   518→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   519→
   520→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   521→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   522→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   523→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   524→
   525→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   526→
   527→    # Policy must be computed after the without-policy steps, which mutate
   528→    # plan (reconcile/prune) and state (unscored sync) before policy reads them.
   529→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   530→        runtime,
   531→        plan,
   532→    )
   533→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   534→        plan=plan,
   535→        state=runtime.state,
   536→        target_strict=target_strict,
   537→        policy=policy,
   538→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   539→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   540→    ) or dirty
   541→
   542→    if dirty:
   543→        try:
   544→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   545→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   546→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   547→
```

```
    30→def resolve_interface(
    31→    explicit: str | None = None,
    32→    install: SkillInstall | None = None,
    33→) -> str | None:
    34→    """Resolve which interface to update."""

    37→

    39→        install = find_installed_skill()
    40→    if not install:
    41→        return None
    42→
    43→    if install.overlay:
    44→        return install.overlay.lower()
    45→
    46→    for name, (target, _overlay, _ded) in SKILL_TARGETS.items():
    47→        if target == install.rel_path:
    48→            return name
    49→    return None
    50→
    51→
    52→def update_installed_skill(interface: str) -> bool:
    53→    """Download and install the skill document for the given interface."""
    54→    target_rel, overlay_name, dedicated = SKILL_TARGETS[interface]
    55→    target_path = get_project_root() / target_rel
    56→
    57→    print(colorize(f"Downloading skill document ({interface})...", "dim"))
    58→    try:
    59→        skill_content = _download("SKILL.md")
    60→        overlay_content = _download(f"{overlay_name}.md") if overlay_name else None
    61→    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError) as exc:
    62→        print(colorize(f"Download failed: {exc}", "red"))
    63→        return False
    64→
    65→    if "desloppify-skill-version" not in skill_content:
    66→        print(colorize("Downloaded content doesn't look like a skill document.", "red"))
    67→        return False
    68→
    69→    new_section = _build_section(skill_content, overlay_content)
    70→    if interface in _FRONTMATTER_FIRST_INTERFACES:
    71→        new_section = _ensure_frontmatter_first(new_section)
    72→
    73→    target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    74→    if dedicated:
    75→        result = new_section
    76→    elif target_path.is_file():
    77→        existing = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    78→        result = _replace_section(existing, new_section)
    79→    else:
    80→        result = new_section
    81→
    82→    safe_write_text(target_path, result)
    83→
    84→    version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(new_section)
    85→    version = version_match.group(1) if version_match else "?"

    90→        )
    91→    )
    92→    return True
```

```
1→"""Project policy — persistent project-specific rules for triage and review."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→def _default_policy_path() -> Path:
    15→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "project_policy.json"
    16→
    17→
    18→def load_policy(path: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    19→    p = path or _default_policy_path()
    20→    if not p.exists():
    21→        return {"rules": []}
    22→    try:
    23→        data = json.loads(p.read_text())
    24→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
    25→        return {"rules": []}
    26→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    27→        return {"rules": []}
    28→    data.setdefault("rules", [])
    29→    return data
    30→
    31→
    32→def save_policy(policy: dict[str, Any], path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    33→    p = path or _default_policy_path()
    34→    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    35→    safe_write_text(p, json.dumps(policy, indent=2) + "\n")
    36→
    37→
    38→def add_rule(policy: dict[str, Any], text: str) -> int:
    39→    """Add a rule, return its 1-based index."""
    40→    rules = policy.setdefault("rules", [])
    41→    rules.append({"text": text, "created_at": utc_now()})
    42→    return len(rules)
    43→
    44→
    45→def remove_rule(policy: dict[str, Any], index: int) -> str | None:
    46→    """Remove a rule by 1-based index, return its text or None."""
    47→    rules = policy.get("rules", [])
    48→    if 1 <= index <= len(rules):
    49→        return rules.pop(index - 1)["text"]
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def render_policy_block(policy: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    54→    """Render rules as a prompt section. Returns empty string if no rules."""
    55→    rules = policy.get("rules", [])
    56→    if not rules:
    57→        return ""
    58→    lines = [
    59→        "## Project Policy\n",
    60→        "The following project-specific rules MUST be respected.",
    61→        "Do NOT suggest or implement changes that violate these rules.",
    62→        "Flag any action step or suggestion that would violate them.\n",
    63→    ]
    64→    for i, rule in enumerate(rules, 1):
    65→        lines.append(f"{i}. {rule['text']}")
    66→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    67→
    68→
    69→__all__ = [
    70→    "add_rule",
    71→    "load_policy",
    72→    "remove_rule",
    73→    "render_policy_block",
    74→    "save_policy",
    75→]
    76→
```

```
1→"""update-skill command: install or update the desloppify skill document."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import urllib.error
     7→import urllib.request
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.skill_docs import (
    10→    SKILL_BEGIN,
    11→    SKILL_END,
    12→    SKILL_TARGETS,
    13→    SKILL_VERSION,
    14→    SKILL_VERSION_RE,
    15→    SkillInstall,
    16→    find_installed_skill,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    19→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    20→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    21→
    22→_RAW_BASE = (
    23→    "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/peteromallet/desloppify/main/docs"
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _download(filename: str) -> str:
    28→    """Download a file from the desloppify docs directory on GitHub."""
    29→    url = f"{_RAW_BASE}/{filename}"
    30→    with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:  # noqa: S310
    31→        return resp.read().decode("utf-8")
    32→
    33→
    34→def _build_section(skill_content: str, overlay_content: str | None) -> str:
    35→    """Assemble the complete skill section from downloaded parts."""
    36→    parts = [skill_content.rstrip()]
    37→    if overlay_content:
    38→        parts.append(overlay_content.rstrip())
    39→    return "\n\n".join(parts) + "\n"
    40→
    41→
    42→# Interfaces whose skill systems parse YAML frontmatter and require ``---``
    43→# to appear on the very first line of the file.
    44→_FRONTMATTER_FIRST_INTERFACES = frozenset({"amp", "codex"})
    45→
    46→
    47→def _ensure_frontmatter_first(content: str) -> str:
    48→    """Move YAML frontmatter to the top if HTML comments precede it.
    49→
    50→    Some skill systems (e.g. AMP) require ``---`` on line 1 for frontmatter
    51→    parsing.  SKILL.md ships with ``<!-- desloppify-begin -->`` and a version
    52→    comment before the ``---`` block.  This function relocates those HTML
    53→    comment lines to just after the closing ``---``.
    54→    """
    55→    lines = content.split("\n")
    56→
    57→    # Find the opening ``---``.
    58→    fm_start = None
    59→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    60→        if line.strip() == "---":
    61→            fm_start = i
    62→            break
    63→    if fm_start is None or fm_start == 0:
    64→        return content  # already fine or no frontmatter
    65→
    66→    # Collect the HTML-comment lines that precede the frontmatter.
    67→    prefix_lines = lines[:fm_start]
    68→
    69→    # Find the closing ``---``.
    70→    fm_end = None
    71→    for i, line in enumerate(lines[fm_start + 1 :], fm_start + 1):
    72→        if line.strip() == "---":
    73→            fm_end = i
    74→            break
    75→    if fm_end is None:
    76→        return content  # malformed frontmatter, leave untouched
    77→
    78→    # Reassemble: frontmatter first, then the prefix lines, then the rest.
    79→    reordered = (
    80→        lines[fm_start : fm_end + 1]
    81→        + prefix_lines
    82→        + lines[fm_end + 1 :]
    83→    )
    84→    return "\n".join(reordered)
    85→
    86→
    87→def _replace_section(file_content: str, new_section: str) -> str:
    88→    """Replace the desloppify section in a shared file, preserving surrounding content.
    89→
    90→    Uses first ``<!-- desloppify-begin -->`` and last ``<!-- desloppify-end -->``
    91→    so the overlay (which also has an end marker) is captured correctly.
    92→    """
    93→    begin = file_content.find(SKILL_BEGIN)
    94→    end = file_content.rfind(SKILL_END)
    95→    if begin == -1 or end == -1:
    96→        # No section markers — append (first install into existing shared file).
    97→        return file_content.rstrip() + "\n\n" + new_section
    98→
    99→    before = file_content[:begin]
   100→    after = file_content[end + len(SKILL_END):]
   101→    before = before.rstrip() + "\n\n" if before.strip() else ""
   102→    after = "\n" + after.lstrip("\n") if after.strip() else "\n"
   103→    return before + new_section + after
   104→
   105→
   106→def resolve_interface(
   107→    explicit: str | None = None,
   108→    install: SkillInstall | None = None,
   109→) -> str | None:
   110→    """Resolve which interface to update.
   111→
   112→    Uses the explicit argument if given, otherwise infers from an existing
   113→    install's overlay marker or file path.
   114→    """
   115→    if explicit:
   116→        return explicit.lower()
   117→
   118→    if install is None:
   119→        install = find_installed_skill()
   120→    if not install:
   121→        return None
   122→
   123→    if install.overlay:
   124→        return install.overlay.lower()
   125→
   126→    for name, (target, _overlay, _ded) in SKILL_TARGETS.items():
   127→        if target == install.rel_path:
   128→            return name
   129→    return None
   130→
   131→
   132→def update_installed_skill(interface: str) -> bool:
   133→    """Download and install the skill document for the given interface.
   134→
   135→    Returns True on success, False on failure. Prints status messages.
   136→    """
   137→    target_rel, overlay_name, dedicated = SKILL_TARGETS[interface]
   138→    target_path = get_project_root() / target_rel
   139→
   140→    print(colorize(f"Downloading skill document ({interface})...", "dim"))
   141→    try:
   142→        skill_content = _download("SKILL.md")
   143→        overlay_content = _download(f"{overlay_name}.md") if overlay_name else None
   144→    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError) as exc:
   145→        print(colorize(f"Download failed: {exc}", "red"))
   146→        return False
   147→
   148→    if "desloppify-skill-version" not in skill_content:
   149→        print(colorize("Downloaded content doesn't look like a skill document.", "red"))
   150→        return False
   151→
   152→    new_section = _build_section(skill_content, overlay_content)
   153→    if interface in _FRONTMATTER_FIRST_INTERFACES:
   154→        new_section = _ensure_frontmatter_first(new_section)
   155→
   156→    target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   157→    if dedicated:
   158→        result = new_section
   159→    elif target_path.is_file():
   160→        existing = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   161→        result = _replace_section(existing, new_section)
   162→    else:
   163→        result = new_section
   164→
   165→    safe_write_text(target_path, result)
   166→
   167→    version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(new_section)
   168→    version = version_match.group(1) if version_match else "?"
   169→    print(
   170→        colorize(
   171→            f"Updated {target_rel} (v{version}, tool expects v{SKILL_VERSION})",
   172→            "green",
   173→        )
   174→    )
   175→    return True
   176→
   177→
   178→def cmd_update_skill(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   179→    """Install or update the desloppify skill document."""
   180→    interface = resolve_interface(getattr(args, "interface", None))
   181→
   182→    if not interface:
   183→        print(colorize("No installed skill document found.", "yellow"))
   184→        print()
   185→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   186→        print(f"Install with: desloppify update-skill <{names}>")
   187→        return
   188→
   189→    if interface not in SKILL_TARGETS:
   190→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   191→        print(colorize(f"Unknown interface '{interface}'.", "red"))
   192→        print(f"Available: {names}")
   193→        return
   194→
   195→    update_installed_skill(interface)
   196→
```

```
1→"""Authorization and RLS signals for review context."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.signal_patterns import (
    10→    AUTH_GUARD_TOKEN_RE,
    11→    SERVICE_ROLE_TOKEN_RE,
    12→    is_server_only_path,
    13→)
    14→
    15→_ROUTE_AUTH_RE = re.compile(
    16→    r"@(?:app|router|api)\.(?:get|post|put|patch|delete|route)\b"
    17→    r"|app\.(?:get|post|put|patch|delete)\("
    18→    r"|export\s+(?:async\s+)?function\s+(?:GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE)\b"
    19→    r"|@router\.(?:get|post|put|patch|delete)\b",
    20→    re.MULTILINE,
    21→)
    22→_AUTH_GUARD_RE = AUTH_GUARD_TOKEN_RE
    23→_AUTH_USAGE_RE = re.compile(r"\buseAuth\b|\brequest\.user\b|\bsession\.user\b|\bgetUser\b")
    24→_PUBLIC_ROUTE_RE = re.compile(
    25→    r"\b(?:allow_anonymous|allowanonymous|permit_all|public[_\s-]?route|public_endpoint)\b|@\s*public\b",
    26→    re.IGNORECASE,
    27→)
    28→_AUTH_SOURCE_EXTENSIONS = frozenset(
    29→    {
    30→        ".c",
    31→        ".cc",
    32→        ".cpp",
    33→        ".cs",
    34→        ".go",
    35→        ".java",
    36→        ".js",
    37→        ".jsx",
    38→        ".kt",
    39→        ".mjs",
    40→        ".php",
    41→        ".py",
    42→        ".rb",
    43→        ".rs",
    44→        ".sql",
    45→        ".swift",
    46→        ".ts",
    47→        ".tsx",
    48→    }
    49→)
    50→# Table name pattern: matches unquoted, "double-quoted", `backtick`, or [bracket] names,
    51→# optionally preceded by a schema qualifier (e.g. public.users, "auth"."profiles").
    52→_SQL_IDENT = r'(?:"[^"]+"|`[^`]+`|\[[^\]]+\]|\w+)'
    53→_SCHEMA_QUALIFIED_IDENT = rf"(?:{_SQL_IDENT}\.)?({_SQL_IDENT})"
    54→_RLS_TABLE_RE = re.compile(
    55→    rf"CREATE\s+TABLE\s+(?:IF\s+NOT\s+EXISTS\s+)?{_SCHEMA_QUALIFIED_IDENT}",
    56→    re.IGNORECASE,
    57→)
    58→_RLS_ENABLE_RE = re.compile(
    59→    rf"ALTER\s+TABLE\s+{_SCHEMA_QUALIFIED_IDENT}\s+ENABLE\s+ROW\s+LEVEL\s+SECURITY"
    60→    rf"|CREATE\s+POLICY\s+{_SQL_IDENT}\s+ON\s+{_SCHEMA_QUALIFIED_IDENT}",
    61→    re.IGNORECASE,
    62→)
    63→_SUPABASE_CLIENT_RE = re.compile(r"\bcreateClient\b")
    64→
    65→
    66→def _normalize_sql_ident(raw: str) -> str:
    67→    """Strip surrounding quotes/brackets from a SQL identifier for comparison."""
    68→    if len(raw) >= 2:
    69→        if (raw[0] == '"' and raw[-1] == '"') or (raw[0] == '`' and raw[-1] == '`'):
    70→            return raw[1:-1]
    71→        if raw[0] == '[' and raw[-1] == ']':
    72→            return raw[1:-1]
    73→    return raw
    74→
    75→
    76→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    77→class RouteAuthCoverage:
    78→    handlers: int
    79→    with_auth: int
    80→    without_auth: int
    81→    public_routes: int = 0
    82→
    83→    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, int]:
    84→        return {
    85→            "handlers": self.handlers,
    86→            "with_auth": self.with_auth,
    87→            "[REDACTED],
    88→            "public_routes": self.public_routes,
    89→        }
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    93→class AuthorizationSignals:
    94→    """Canonical authorization-signal payload shared by review context paths."""
    95→
    96→    route_auth_coverage: dict[str, RouteAuthCoverage] = field(default_factory=dict)
    97→    rls_with: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    98→    rls_without: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    99→    rls_files: dict[str, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
   100→    service_role_usage: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   101→    auth_patterns: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
   102→    auth_guard_patterns: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
   103→    auth_usage_patterns: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
   104→
   105→    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
   106→        payload: dict[str, object] = {}
   107→        if self.route_auth_coverage:
   108→            payload["route_auth_coverage"] = {
   109→                path: coverage.as_dict()
   110→                for path, coverage in sorted(self.route_auth_coverage.items())
   111→            }
   112→        if self.rls_with or self.rls_without:
   113→            rls_payload: dict[str, object] = {
   114→                "with_rls": self.rls_with,
   115→                "without_rls": self.rls_without,
   116→            }
   117→            if self.rls_files:
   118→                rls_payload["files"] = self.rls_files
   119→            payload["rls_coverage"] = rls_payload
   120→        if self.service_role_usage:
   121→            payload["service_role_usage"] = self.service_role_usage
   122→        if self.auth_patterns:
   123→            payload["auth_patterns"] = self.auth_patterns
   124→        if self.auth_guard_patterns:
   125→            payload["auth_guard_patterns"] = self.auth_guard_patterns
   126→        if self.auth_usage_patterns:
   127→            payload["auth_usage_patterns"] = self.auth_usage_patterns
   128→        return payload
   129→
   130→
   131→def gather_auth_context(
   132→    file_contents: dict[str, str],
   133→    *,
   134→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
   135→) -> dict[str, object]:
   136→    """Compute auth/RLS context from file contents.
   137→
   138→    Returns route auth coverage, RLS coverage, service role usage, and auth patterns.
   139→    """
   140→    route_auth: dict[str, RouteAuthCoverage] = {}
   141→    rls_tables: set[str] = set()
   142→    rls_enabled: set[str] = set()
   143→    rls_table_files: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   144→    service_role_files: set[str] = set()
   145→    auth_patterns: dict[str, int] = {}
   146→    auth_guard_patterns: dict[str, int] = {}
   147→    auth_usage_patterns: dict[str, int] = {}
   148→
   149→    for filepath, content in file_contents.items():
   150→        if not _is_auth_source_file(filepath):
   151→            continue
   152→        rpath = rel_fn(filepath)
   153→
   154→        # Route auth coverage
   155→        route_segments = _route_segments(content)
   156→        if route_segments:
   157→            handler_count = len(route_segments)
   158→            auth_count = 0
   159→            public_count = 0
   160→            for segment in route_segments:
   161→                if _AUTH_GUARD_RE.search(segment):
   162→                    auth_count += 1
   163→                elif _PUBLIC_ROUTE_RE.search(segment):
   164→                    public_count += 1
   165→            route_auth[rpath] = RouteAuthCoverage(
   166→                handlers=handler_count,
   167→                with_auth=auth_count,
   168→                without_auth=max(0, handler_count - auth_count - public_count),
   169→                public_routes=public_count,
   170→            )
   171→
   172→        # RLS coverage (SQL/migration files)
   173→        for match in _RLS_TABLE_RE.finditer(content):
   174→            table = _normalize_sql_ident(match.group(1))
   175→            rls_tables.add(table)
   176→            rls_table_files.setdefault(table, []).append(rpath)
   177→        for match in _RLS_ENABLE_RE.finditer(content):
   178→            table = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
   179→            if table:
   180→                rls_enabled.add(_normalize_sql_ident(table))
   181→
   182→        # Service role usage
   183→        if (
   184→            SERVICE_ROLE_TOKEN_RE.search(content)
   185→            and _SUPABASE_CLIENT_RE.search(content)
   186→            and not is_server_only_path(filepath)
   187→        ):
   188→            service_role_files.add(rpath)
   189→
   190→        # Auth check patterns
   191→        guard_count = len(_AUTH_GUARD_RE.findall(content))
   192→        usage_count = len(_AUTH_USAGE_RE.findall(content))
   193→        if guard_count > 0:
   194→            auth_guard_patterns[rpath] = guard_count
   195→        if usage_count > 0:
   196→            auth_usage_patterns[rpath] = usage_count
   197→        total_auth_signals = guard_count + usage_count
   198→        if total_auth_signals > 0:
   199→            auth_patterns[rpath] = total_auth_signals
   200→
   201→    tables_without_rls = rls_tables - rls_enabled
   202→    rls_files: dict[str, list[str]] = {
   203→        table: sorted(set(rls_table_files.get(table, [])))
   204→        for table in sorted(tables_without_rls)
   205→        if table in rls_table_files
   206→    }
   207→    return AuthorizationSignals(
   208→        route_auth_coverage=route_auth,
   209→        rls_with=sorted(rls_tables & rls_enabled),
   210→        rls_without=sorted(tables_without_rls),
   211→        rls_files=rls_files,
   212→        service_role_usage=sorted(service_role_files),
   213→        auth_patterns=auth_patterns,
   214→        auth_guard_patterns=auth_guard_patterns,
   215→        auth_usage_patterns=auth_usage_patterns,
   216→    ).as_dict()
   217→
   218→
   219→def _route_segments(content: str) -> list[str]:
   220→    """Split source into route-handler scoped segments."""
   221→    matches = list(_ROUTE_AUTH_RE.finditer(content))
   222→    if not matches:
   223→        return []
   224→    segments: list[str] = []
   225→    for idx, match in enumerate(matches):
   226→        start = match.start()
   227→        end = matches[idx + 1].start() if idx + 1 < len(matches) else len(content)
   228→        segments.append(content[start:end])
   229→    return segments
   230→
   231→
   232→def _is_auth_source_file(filepath: str) -> bool:
   233→    """Return True when a file path should be scanned for executable auth signals."""
   234→    lower = filepath.lower()
   235→    return any(lower.endswith(ext) for ext in _AUTH_SOURCE_EXTENSIONS)
   236→
   237→
   238→__all__ = ["AuthorizationSignals", "RouteAuthCoverage", "gather_auth_context"]
   239→
```

```
1→"""File-level TypeScript/SQL security checks."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.security.entries import _make_security_entry
     8→from desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.security.patterns import (
     9→    _AUTH_CHECK_RE,
    10→    _CREATE_VIEW_RE,
    11→    _EDGE_ENTRYPOINT_RE,
    12→    _JSON_DEEP_CLONE_RE,
    13→    _JSON_PARSE_RE,
    14→    _SECURITY_INVOKER_RE,
    15→    _SERVE_ASYNC_RE,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _file_level_security_issues(
    20→    *,
    21→    filepath: str,
    22→    normalized_path: str,
    23→    lines: list[str],
    24→    content: str,
    25→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    26→    """Detect file-level security patterns and return issues."""
    27→    file_issues: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    28→
    29→    if _looks_like_edge_handler(normalized_path, content):
    30→        if not _handler_has_auth_check(content):
    31→            file_issues.append(
    32→                _make_security_entry(
    33→                    filepath,
    34→                    1,
    35→                    content.splitlines()[0] if lines else "",
    36→                    check_id="edge_function_missing_auth",
    37→                    summary="Edge function serves requests without authentication check",
    38→                    severity="high",
    39→                    confidence="medium",
    40→                    remediation="Add authentication check (e.g., requireAuth, getServerSession, authenticateRequest, auth.getUser, verifyToken)",
    41→                )
    42→            )
    43→
    44→    _check_json_parse_unguarded(filepath, lines, file_issues)
    45→    if filepath.endswith(".sql"):
    46→        _check_rls_bypass(filepath, content, lines, file_issues)
    47→    return file_issues
    48→
    49→
    50→def _looks_like_edge_handler(normalized_path: str, content: str) -> bool:
    51→    """Detect edge-function style handlers without relying on index.ts naming."""
    52→    in_edge_tree = "/functions/" in normalized_path.replace("\\", "/")
    53→    has_edge_entrypoint = bool(
    54→        _SERVE_ASYNC_RE.search(content) or _EDGE_ENTRYPOINT_RE.search(content)
    55→    )
    56→    return in_edge_tree and has_edge_entrypoint
    57→
    58→
    59→def _extract_handler_body(content: str) -> str | None:
    60→    """Extract body of first serve() or exported handler function."""
    61→    match = _SERVE_ASYNC_RE.search(content) or _EDGE_ENTRYPOINT_RE.search(content)
    62→    if not match:
    63→        return None
    64→
    65→    start = match.end()
    66→    brace_pos = content.find("{", start)
    67→    if brace_pos == -1:
    68→        return None
    69→
    70→    depth = 0
    71→    for i in range(brace_pos, len(content)):
    72→        ch = content[i]
    73→        if ch == "{":
    74→            depth += 1
    75→        elif ch == "}":
    76→            depth -= 1
    77→            if depth == 0:
    78→                return content[brace_pos : i + 1]
    79→    return None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _handler_has_auth_check(content: str) -> bool:
    83→    """Check if auth patterns exist inside handler body, not just file-level."""
    84→    handler_body = _extract_handler_body(content)
    85→    if handler_body is None:
    86→        return bool(_AUTH_CHECK_RE.search(content))
    87→    return bool(_AUTH_CHECK_RE.search(handler_body))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _is_in_try_scope(lines: list[str], target_line: int) -> bool:
    91→    """Check if target_line (1-indexed) is inside a try block by scanning backwards."""
    92→    depth = 0
    93→    for i in range(target_line - 2, -1, -1):
    94→        stripped = lines[i].strip()
    95→        if re.match(r"(?:async\s+)?function\b", stripped):
    96→            return False
    97→        depth += stripped.count("}") - stripped.count("{")
    98→        if depth <= 0 and re.search(r"\btry\b", stripped):
    99→            return True
   100→    return False
   101→
   102→
   103→def _check_json_parse_unguarded(
   104→    filepath: str,
   105→    lines: list[str],
   106→    entries: list[dict[str, object]],
   107→) -> None:
   108→    """Check for JSON.parse not inside a try block."""
   109→    for line_num, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
   110→        if not _JSON_PARSE_RE.search(line):
   111→            continue
   112→        stripped = line.lstrip()
   113→        if stripped.startswith("//"):
   114→            continue
   115→        if _JSON_DEEP_CLONE_RE.search(line):
   116→            continue
   117→        if _is_in_try_scope(lines, line_num):
   118→            continue
   119→        entries.append(
   120→            _make_security_entry(
   121→                filepath,
   122→                line_num,
   123→                line,
   124→                check_id="json_parse_unguarded",
   125→                summary="JSON.parse() without try/catch - may throw on malformed input",
   126→                severity="low",
   127→                confidence="low",
   128→                remediation="Wrap JSON.parse() in a try/catch block",
   129→            )
   130→        )
   131→
   132→
   133→def _check_rls_bypass(
   134→    filepath: str,
   135→    content: str,
   136→    lines: list[str],
   137→    entries: list[dict[str, object]],
   138→) -> None:
   139→    """Check for CREATE VIEW without security_invoker in SQL files."""
   140→    for match in _CREATE_VIEW_RE.finditer(content):
   141→        line_num = content[: match.start()].count("\n") + 1
   142→        view_block = content[match.start() : match.start() + 500]
   143→        if _SECURITY_INVOKER_RE.search(view_block):
   144→            continue
   145→        entries.append(
   146→            _make_security_entry(
   147→                filepath,
   148→                line_num,
   149→                lines[line_num - 1] if 0 < line_num <= len(lines) else "",
   150→                check_id="rls_bypass_views",
   151→                summary="SQL VIEW without security_invoker=true may bypass RLS",
   152→                severity="high",
   153→                confidence="medium",
   154→                remediation="Add 'WITH (security_invoker = true)' to the view definition",
   155→            )
   156→        )
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "_check_json_parse_unguarded",
   161→    "_check_rls_bypass",
   162→    "_extract_handler_body",
   163→    "_file_level_security_issues",
   164→    "_handler_has_auth_check",
   165→    "_is_in_try_scope",
   166→    "_looks_like_edge_handler",
   167→]
   168→
```

```
    56→def _auto_confirm_stage(
    57→    *,
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    stage_record: dict,
    60→    stage_name: str,
    61→    stage_label: str,
    62→    attestation: str | None,
    63→    blocked_heading: str,
    64→    confirm_cmd: str,
    65→    inline_hint: str,
    66→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    67→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    68→    save_plan_fn=None,
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """Shared auto-confirm flow for stage fold-confirm operations."""
    71→    if save_plan_fn is None:
    72→        save_plan_fn = save_plan
    73→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    74→        return True
    75→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  {blocked_heading}", "red"))
    77→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
    78→        print(colorize(f"  {inline_hint}", "dim"))
    79→        return False
    80→
    81→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
    82→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
    83→        confirmed_text,
    84→        stage_name,
    85→        dimensions=dimensions,
    86→        cluster_names=cluster_names,
    87→    )
    88→    if validation_err:
    89→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
    90→        return False
    91→
    92→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
    93→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
    94→    save_plan_fn(plan)
    95→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   100→    *,
   101→    plan: dict,
   102→    stages: dict,
   103→    attestation: str | None,
   104→    triage_input,
   105→    save_plan_fn=None,
   106→) -> bool:
   107→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe")
   108→    if observe_stage is None:
   109→        return False
   110→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   111→    return _auto_confirm_stage(
   112→        plan=plan,
   113→        stage_record=observe_stage,
   114→        stage_name="observe",
   115→        stage_label="Observe",
   116→        attestation=attestation,
   117→        blocked_heading="Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.",
   118→        confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm observe",
   119→        inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.",
   120→        dimensions=dim_names,
   121→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   126→    *,
   127→    report: str,
   128→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   129→    recurring: dict,
   130→) -> bool:
   131→    if not recurring_dims:
   132→        return True
   133→    report_lower = report.lower()
   134→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
   135→    if mentioned:
   136→        return True
   137→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
   138→    for dim in recurring_dims:
   139→        info = recurring[dim]
   140→        print(
   141→            colorize(
   142→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   143→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   144→                "yellow",
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    print(colorize("  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.", "dim"))
   148→    return False
   149→
   150→
   151→def _analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   152→    *,
   153→    report: str,
   154→    valid_ids: set[str],
   155→) -> tuple[set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   156→    """Return cited, missing, and duplicate issue IDs referenced by reflect."""
   157→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   158→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str] = {}
   159→    for issue_id in sorted(valid_ids):
   160→        short_id = issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   161→        short_id_buckets.setdefault(short_id, []).append(issue_id)
   162→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", short_id):
   163→            existing = short_hex_map.get(short_id)
   164→            if existing is None:
   165→                short_hex_map[short_id] = issue_id
   166→            elif existing != issue_id:
   167→                # Ambiguous hex short IDs cannot be resolved safely.
   168→                short_hex_map.pop(short_id, None)
   169→
   170→    def _extract_ledger_key(line: str) -> str | None:
   171→        match = re.match(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->", line)
   172→        if not match:
   173→            return None
   174→        token = match.group(1).strip().strip("`").strip()
   175→        if token.startswith("[") and token.endswith("]"):
   176→            token = token[1:-1].strip()
   177→        return token or None
   178→
   179→    ledger_hits: Counter[str] | None = None
   180→    short_id_usage: Counter[str] = Counter()
   181→    in_ledger = False
   182→    for raw_line in report.splitlines():
   183→        line = raw_line.strip()
   184→        if re.fullmatch(r"##\s+Coverage Ledger", line, re.IGNORECASE):
   185→            in_ledger = True
   186→            ledger_hits = Counter()
   187→            continue
   188→        if in_ledger and re.match(r"##\s+", line):
   189→            break
   190→        if not in_ledger:
   191→            continue
   192→        [REDACTED](line)
   193→        if token:
   194→            if token in valid_ids:
   195→                ledger_hits[token] += 1
   196→                continue
   197→            bucket = short_id_buckets.get(token)
   198→            if bucket:
   199→                bucket_index = short_id_usage[token]
   200→                issue_id = bucket[bucket_index] if bucket_index < len(bucket) else bucket[-1]
   201→                short_id_usage[token] += 1
   202→                ledger_hits[issue_id] += 1
   203→                continue
   204→        for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", line):
   205→            issue_id = short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   206→            if issue_id:
   207→                ledger_hits[issue_id] += 1
   208→    if ledger_hits is not None and ledger_hits:
   209→        cited = set(ledger_hits)
   210→        duplicates = sorted(issue_id for issue_id, count in ledger_hits.items() if count > 1)
   211→        missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   212→        return cited, missing, duplicates
   213→    cited = extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   214→    # Also match valid IDs that appear literally in the report
   215→    # (handles non-hex IDs like test fixtures).
   216→    for issue_id in valid_ids:
   217→        if issue_id in report:
   218→            cited.add(issue_id)
   219→    short_hits = Counter(
   220→        short_hex_map[token]
   221→        for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", report)
   222→        if token in short_hex_map
   223→    )
   224→    duplicates = sorted(issue_id for issue_id, count in short_hits.items() if count > 1)
   225→    missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   226→    return cited, missing, duplicates
   227→
   228→
   229→def _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   230→    *,
   231→    report: str,
   232→    valid_ids: set[str],
   233→) -> tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   234→    """Ensure the reflect blueprint accounts for every open review issue exactly once."""
   235→    cited, missing, duplicates = _analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   236→        report=report,
   237→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   238→    )
   239→    if not missing and not duplicates:
   240→        return True, cited, missing, duplicates
   241→    print(
   242→        colorize(
   243→            "  Reflect report must account for every open review issue exactly once.",
   244→            "red",
   245→        )
   246→    )
   247→    if missing:
   248→        missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing[:10])
   249→        print(colorize(f"    Missing: {missing_short}", "yellow"))
   250→    if duplicates:
   251→        duplicate_short = ", ".join(
   252→            issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicates[:10]
   253→        )
   254→        print(colorize(f"    Duplicated: {duplicate_short}", "yellow"))
   255→    print(colorize("  Fix the reflect blueprint before running organize.", "dim"))
   256→    return False, cited, missing, duplicates
   257→
   258→
   259→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
   260→    if "reflect" in stages:
   261→        return True
   262→    if "observe" not in stages:
   263→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   264→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   265→        return False
   266→    print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   267→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   268→    return False
   269→
   270→
   271→def _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   272→    *,
   273→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   274→    plan: dict,
   275→    stages: dict,
   276→    attestation: str | None,
   277→    triage_input=None,
   278→    command_runtime_fn=None,
   279→    collect_triage_input_fn=collect_triage_input,
   280→    detect_recurring_patterns_fn=detect_recurring_patterns,
   281→    save_plan_fn=None,
   282→) -> bool:
   283→    reflect_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   284→    if reflect_stage is None:
   285→        return False
   286→
   287→    resolved_triage_input = triage_input
   288→    if resolved_triage_input is None:
   289→        runtime_factory = command_runtime_fn or command_runtime
   290→        runtime = runtime_factory(args)
   291→        resolved_triage_input = collect_triage_input_fn(plan, runtime.state)
   292→
   293→    valid_ids = set(resolved_triage_input.open_issues.keys())
   294→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   295→        report=str(reflect_stage.get("report", "")),
   296→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   297→    )
   298→    if not accounting_ok:
   299→        return False
   300→    reflect_stage["cited_ids"] = sorted(cited_ids)
   301→    reflect_stage["missing_issue_ids"] = missing_ids
   302→    reflect_stage["duplicate_issue_ids"] = duplicate_ids
   303→
   304→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns_fn(
   305→        resolved_triage_input.open_issues,
   306→        resolved_triage_input.resolved_issues,
   307→    )
   308→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(resolved_triage_input)
   309→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   310→    reflect_clusters = [name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")]
   311→    return _auto_confirm_stage(
   312→        plan=plan,
   313→        stage_record=reflect_stage,
   314→        stage_name="reflect",
   315→        stage_label="Reflect",
   316→        attestation=attestation,
   317→        blocked_heading="Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.",
   318→        confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect",
   319→        inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.",
   320→        dimensions=reflect_dims,
   321→        cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   322→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   323→    )
   324→
   325→
   326→def _manual_clusters_or_error(plan: dict) -> list[str] | None:
   327→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   328→    if manual_clusters:
   329→        return manual_clusters
   330→    any_clusters = [name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if cluster.get("issue_ids")]
   331→    if any_clusters:
   332→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
   333→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
   334→    else:
   335→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   336→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   337→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
   338→    return None
   339→
   340→
   341→def _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan: dict) -> bool:
   342→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   343→    if not gaps:
   344→        return True
   345→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
   346→    for name, missing in gaps:
   347→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   348→    print()
   349→    print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
   350→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" --steps "step 1" "step 2"', "dim"))
   351→    return False
   352→
   353→
   354→def _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   355→    """Block if open review issues aren't in any manual cluster. Return True if OK."""
   356→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   357→    if not unclustered:
   358→        return True
   359→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no cluster.", "red"))
   360→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   361→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   362→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   363→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
   364→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   365→    print()
   366→    print(colorize("  Every review issue needs an action plan. Either:", "dim"))
   367→    print(colorize("    1. Add to a cluster: desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern>", "dim"))
   368→    print(colorize('    2. Wontfix it: desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "reason" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   369→    return False
   370→
   371→
   372→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   373→    if not report:
   374→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   375→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   376→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   377→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   378→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   379→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   380→        return None
   381→    if len(report) < 100:
   382→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   383→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   384→        return None
   385→    return report
   386→
```

```
    39→def _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
    40→    *,
    41→    plan: dict,
    42→    stages: dict,
    43→    stage: str,
    44→    attestation: str | None,
    45→    save_plan_fn=None,
    46→) -> bool:

    49→    stage_record = stages.get(stage)

    56→        stage_record=stage_record,
    57→        stage_name=stage,
    58→        stage_label=config["label"],
    59→        attestation=attestation,
    60→        blocked_heading=config["blocked_heading"],
    61→        confirm_cmd=config["confirm_cmd"],
    62→        inline_hint=config["inline_hint"],
    63→        cluster_names=_manual_cluster_names(plan),
    64→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
    69→    *,
    70→    plan: dict,
    71→    meta: dict,
    72→    stages: dict,
    73→) -> bool:
    74→    if "enrich" in stages:
    75→        return True
    76→    if "organize" not in stages:
    77→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
    78→
    79→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    80→    if underspec:
    81→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
    82→        print(colorize(f"  {len(underspec)} cluster(s) have underspecified steps (missing detail or issue_refs):", "yellow"))
    83→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
    84→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
    85→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
    86→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    87→    else:
    88→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
    89→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
    90→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    91→    return False
    92→
    93→
    94→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
    95→    *,
    96→    plan: dict,
    97→    stages: dict,
    98→    attestation: str | None,
    99→    save_plan_fn=None,
   100→) -> bool:
   101→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   102→        return False
   103→
   104→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   105→    if underspec:
   106→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   107→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   108→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
   109→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   110→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   111→        return False
   112→
   113→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   114→        plan=plan,
   115→        stages=stages,
   116→        stage="enrich",
   117→        attestation=attestation,
   118→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→def _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(
   123→    *,
   124→    plan: dict,
   125→    meta: dict,
   126→    stages: dict,
   127→) -> bool:
   128→    if "sense-check" in stages:
   129→        return True
   130→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   131→        return _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   132→
   133→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: sense-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   134→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   135→    return False
   136→
   137→
   138→def _auto_confirm_sense_check_for_complete(
   139→    *,
   140→    plan: dict,
   141→    stages: dict,
   142→    attestation: str | None,
   143→    save_plan_fn=None,
   144→) -> bool:
   145→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   146→        plan=plan,
   147→        stages=stages,
   148→        stage="sense-check",
   149→        attestation=attestation,
   150→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   155→    *,
   156→    plan: dict,
   157→    meta: dict,
   158→    stages: dict,
   159→) -> bool:
   160→    if "organize" in stages:
   161→        return True
   162→    if "observe" not in stages:
   163→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   164→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   165→        return False
   166→
   167→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   168→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   169→    if gaps:
   170→        print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   171→        for name, missing in gaps:
   172→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   173→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
   174→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   175→    else:
   176→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   177→        if manual:
   178→            print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   179→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   180→        else:
   181→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   182→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   183→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   184→        print(colorize('  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   185→    return False
   186→
   187→
   188→def _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
   189→    *,
   190→    plan: dict,
   191→    stages: dict,
   192→    attestation: str | None,
   193→    save_plan_fn=None,
   194→) -> bool:
   195→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   196→        plan=plan,
   197→        stages=stages,
   198→        stage="organize",
   199→        attestation=attestation,
   200→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→__all__ = [
   205→    "_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete",
   206→    "_auto_confirm_organize_for_complete",
   207→    "_auto_confirm_sense_check_for_complete",
   208→    "_require_enrich_stage_for_complete",
   209→    "_require_organize_stage_for_complete",
   210→    "_require_sense_check_stage_for_complete",
   211→]
   212→
```

```
1→"""Prompt/data contracts for whole-plan triage orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field

     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,

    12→    triage_clusters,
```
