    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data needed to produce/update triage clusters (legacy schema)."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # legacy field name; values are clusters
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→    @property
    33→    def existing_clusters(self) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
    34→        """Canonical alias for ``existing_epics`` used by staged triage docs."""
    35→        return self.existing_epics
    36→
    37→@dataclass
    38→class DismissedIssue:
    39→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    40→
    41→    issue_id: str
    42→    reason: str
    43→
    44→@dataclass
    45→class ContradictionNote:
    46→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    47→
    48→    kept: str
    49→    dismissed: str
    50→    reason: str
    51→
    52→@dataclass
    53→class TriageResult:
    54→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    55→
    56→    strategy_summary: str
    57→    epics: list[dict]
    58→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    59→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    60→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    61→
    62→
    63→def _issue_dimension(issue: dict) -> str:
    64→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    65→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    66→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    67→        if isinstance(dimension, str):
    68→            return dimension
    69→    return ""
    70→
    71→
    72→def _recurring_dimensions(
    73→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    74→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    75→) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]]:
    76→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    77→    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():
    78→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    79→        if dimension:
    80→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    81→
    82→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    83→    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():
    84→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    85→        if dimension:
    86→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    87→
    88→    recurring: dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]] = {}
    89→    for dimension in sorted(set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim)):
    90→        recurring[dimension] = {
    91→            "open": sorted(open_by_dim[dimension]),
    92→            "resolved": sorted(resolved_by_dim[dimension]),
    93→        }
    94→    return recurring
    95→
    96→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
    97→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
    98→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    99→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   100→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   101→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
   102→
   103→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   104→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   105→    for fid, f in issues.items():
   106→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   107→            continue
   108→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   109→            open_review[fid] = f
   110→        else:
   111→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   112→
   113→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   114→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   115→    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   116→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
   117→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   118→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   119→
   120→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
   121→    resolved_issue_objs = {
   122→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
   123→    }
   124→
   125→    # Completed clusters since last triage completion
   126→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   127→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   128→    if last_completed:
   129→        recent_completed = [
   130→            c for c in all_completed
   131→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   132→        ]
   133→    else:
   134→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
   135→
   136→    return TriageInput(
   137→        open_issues=open_review,
   138→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   139→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   140→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   141→        new_since_last=new_since,
   142→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   143→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   144→        triage_version=version,
   145→        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,
   146→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   147→    )
   148→
   149→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   150→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. This is the legacy
   151→whole-plan triage contract used by compatibility callers; the primary runtime
   152→uses staged triage commands. Produce a coherent prioritized strategy for all
   153→open review issues.
   154→
   155→Your plan should:
   156→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   157→- Give each cluster (``epics`` field in this legacy schema) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   158→- Order clusters by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   159→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   160→- Mark which clusters are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   161→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   162→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   163→
   164→Available directions for clusters: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   165→
   166→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   167→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   168→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   169→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   170→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   171→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   172→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   173→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   174→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   175→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   176→
   177→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any cluster
   178→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   179→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   180→
   181→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   182→{
   183→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   184→  "epics": [
   185→    {
   186→      "name": "slug-name",
   187→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   188→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   189→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   190→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   191→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   192→      "agent_safe": true,
   193→      "dependency_order": 1,
   194→      "action_steps": [
   195→        {
   196→          "title": "Short imperative step title",
   197→          "detail": "Concrete implementation detail with file paths/locations",
   198→          "issue_refs": ["id1"]
   199→        }
   200→      ],
   201→      "status": "pending"
   202→    }
   203→  ],
   204→  "dismissed_issues": [
   205→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   206→  ],
   207→  "contradiction_notes": [
   208→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   209→  ],
   210→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   211→}
   212→"""
   213→
   214→def build_triage_prompt(si: TriageInput) -> str:
   215→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all issue data."""
   216→    parts: list[str] = []
   217→
   218→    # Section: existing clusters (legacy key: existing_epics)
   219→    if si.existing_epics:
   220→        parts.append("## Existing clusters (legacy field: existing_epics)")
   221→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   222→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   223→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   224→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   225→            fids = epic.get("issue_ids", [])
   226→            parts.append(
   227→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   228→                f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   229→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   230→            )
   231→        parts.append("")
   232→
   233→    # Section: what changed
   234→    if si.new_since_last:
   235→        parts.append(f"## New issues since last triage ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   236→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   237→            f = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
   238→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   239→        parts.append("")
   240→
   241→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   242→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last triage ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   243→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   244→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   245→        parts.append("")
   246→
   247→    if si.resolved_issues:
   248→        parts.append(
   249→            "## Resolved review issues available for recurrence context "
   250→            f"({len(si.resolved_issues)})"
   251→        )
   252→        resolved_ids = sorted(si.resolved_issues)
   253→        for fid in resolved_ids[:30]:
   254→            issue = si.resolved_issues.get(fid, {})
   255→            summary = str(issue.get("summary", "(no summary)"))
   256→            dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
   257→            dim_suffix = f" [{dimension}]" if dimension else ""
   258→            parts.append(f"- {fid}{dim_suffix}: {summary}")
   259→        if len(resolved_ids) > 30:
   260→            parts.append(f"- ... and {len(resolved_ids) - 30} more resolved issues")
   261→        parts.append("")
   262→
   263→    if si.completed_clusters:
   264→        parts.append(
   265→            "## Completed clusters since last triage "
   266→            f"({len(si.completed_clusters)})"
   267→        )
   268→        completed = sorted(
   269→            si.completed_clusters,
   270→            key=lambda cluster: str(cluster.get("completed_at", "")),
   271→            reverse=True,
   272→        )
   273→        for cluster in completed[:10]:
   274→            name = str(cluster.get("name", "(unnamed cluster)"))
   275→            thesis = str(cluster.get("thesis") or cluster.get("description") or "")
   276→            completed_at = str(cluster.get("completed_at", ""))
   277→            issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   278→            issue_count = len(issue_ids) if isinstance(issue_ids, list) else 0
   279→            parts.append(
   280→                f"- {name}: {thesis} (issues: {issue_count}, completed_at: {completed_at})"
   281→            )
   282→        if len(completed) > 10:
   283→            parts.append(f"- ... and {len(completed) - 10} more completed clusters")
   284→        parts.append("")
   285→
   286→    recurring = _recurring_dimensions(si.open_issues, si.resolved_issues)
   287→    if recurring:
   288→        parts.append(
   289→            "## Potential recurring dimensions (resolved issues still have open peers)"
   290→        )
   291→        for dimension, bucket in recurring.items():
   292→            open_count = len(bucket["open"])
   293→            resolved_count = len(bucket["resolved"])
   294→            parts.append(
   295→                f"- {dimension}: {open_count} open / {resolved_count} recently resolved"
   296→            )
   297→        parts.append("")
   298→
   299→    # Section: all open review issues
   300→    parts.append(f"## All open review issues ({len(si.open_issues)})")
   301→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_issues.items()):
   302→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   303→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   304→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   305→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   306→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   307→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   308→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   309→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   310→        if dimension:
   311→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   312→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   313→        if suggestion:
   314→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   315→    parts.append("")
   316→
   317→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   318→    if si.dimension_scores:
   319→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   320→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   321→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   322→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   323→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   324→                issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   325→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   326→        parts.append("")
   327→
   328→    # Section: previously dismissed
   329→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   330→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   331→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   332→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   333→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   334→        parts.append("")
   335→
   336→    return "\n".join(parts)
   337→
   338→__all__ = [
   339→    "_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT",
   340→    "ContradictionNote",
   341→    "DismissedIssue",
   342→    "TriageInput",
   343→    "TriageResult",
   344→    "build_triage_prompt",
   345→    "collect_triage_input",
   346→]
   347→
```

    46→
    47→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel, state: dict | None = None) -> str:
    48→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    49→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    50→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    51→    has_triage = any(stage_id in order for stage_id in TRIAGE_IDS)
    52→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    53→    run_hint = (
    54→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    55→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    56→    )
    57→
    58→    if not has_triage:
    59→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    60→            return (
    61→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage. "
    62→                f"{run_hint}"
    63→            )
    64→        return ""
    65→
    66→    if state and has_objective_backlog(state, None):
    67→        return (
    68→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
    69→            "is complete."
    70→        )
    71→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    72→    completed = [
    73→        stage
    74→        for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
    75→        if stage in stages
    76→    ]
    77→    if completed:
    78→        return (
    79→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
    80→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
    81→        )
    82→    return (
    83→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
    84→        f"{run_hint}"
    85→    )
    86→
    87→__all__ = [
    88→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
    89→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
    90→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH",

    25→

    27→RecurringPatternMap = dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]]

    30→class AppendLogEntryFn(Protocol):
    31→    """Typed callable contract for plan execution-log mutation."""
    32→
    33→    def __call__(
    34→        self,
    35→        plan: PlanModel,
    36→        action: str,
    37→        *,
    38→        issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    39→        cluster_name: str | None = None,
    40→        actor: str = "user",
    41→        note: str | None = None,
    42→        detail: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
    43→    ) -> None: ...
    44→
    45→
    46→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    47→class TriageServices:
    48→    """Callables shared across triage handler modules."""
    49→
    50→    command_runtime: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], CommandRuntime]
    51→    load_plan: Callable[[], PlanModel]
    52→    save_plan: Callable[[PlanModel], None]
    53→    collect_triage_input: Callable[[PlanModel, StateModel], TriageInput]
    54→    detect_recurring_patterns: Callable[[IssueMap, IssueMap], RecurringPatternMap]
    55→    append_log_entry: AppendLogEntryFn
    56→    extract_issue_citations: Callable[[str, set[str]], set[str]]
    57→    build_triage_prompt: Callable[[TriageInput], str]
    58→
    59→
    60→def default_triage_services() -> TriageServices:
    61→    """Return the default runtime triage service bundle."""
    62→    return TriageServices(
    63→        command_runtime=command_runtime,
    64→        load_plan=load_plan,
    65→        save_plan=save_plan,

    68→        append_log_entry=append_log_entry,
    69→        extract_issue_citations=extract_issue_citations,
    70→        build_triage_prompt=build_triage_prompt,
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→__all__ = [

1→"""Workflow-item resolution logic for `plan resolve`."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Literal
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_resolve_helpers import blocked_triage_stages

    35→
    36→WORKFLOW_GATE_IDS = frozenset({WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID})
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40→class WorkflowResolveOutcome:
    41→    status: Literal["handled", "blocked", "fall_through"]
    42→    remaining_patterns: list[str]
    43→
    44→
    45→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    46→class StageCoaching:
    47→    headline: str
    48→    details: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    49→    commands: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    50→    footer: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    51→
    52→
    53→STAGE_COACHING = {
    54→    "observe": StageCoaching(
    55→        headline="  You must analyze the findings before resolving this.",
    56→        details=("  Start by examining themes, root causes, and contradictions:",),
    57→        footer=("  The report must be 100+ chars describing what you found.",),
    58→    ),
    59→    "reflect": StageCoaching(
    60→        headline="  Observe is done. Now compare against previously completed work:",
    61→        footer=("  The report must mention recurring dimensions if any exist.",),
    62→    ),
    63→    "organize": StageCoaching(
    64→        headline="  Reflect is done. Now create clusters and prioritize:",
    65→        commands=(
    66→            '    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."',
    67→            "    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>",
    68→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step1" "step2"',
    69→            "    {organize_manual_command}",
    70→        ),
    71→        footer=("  All manual clusters must have descriptions and action_steps.",),
    72→    ),
    73→    "enrich": StageCoaching(
    74→        headline="  Organize is done. Now enrich steps with detail and issue refs:",
    75→        commands=(
    76→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
    77→        ),
    78→    ),
    79→    "commit": StageCoaching(
    80→        headline="  Enrich is done. Finalize the execution plan:",
    81→    ),
    82→}
    83→
    84→
    85→def _print_stage_runner_guidance(next_stage: str) -> None:
    86→    print(colorize("  Preferred runners:", "yellow"))
    87→    runner_commands = (
    88→        triage_runner_commands()
    89→        if next_stage == "commit"
    90→        else triage_runner_commands(only_stages=next_stage)
    91→    )
    92→    for label, command in runner_commands:
    93→        print(colorize(f"    {label}: {command}", "dim"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def _print_stage_coaching(next_stage: str) -> None:
    97→    coaching = STAGE_COACHING.get(next_stage)
    98→    if coaching is None:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    print(colorize(coaching.headline, "yellow"))
   102→    for line in coaching.details:
   103→        print(colorize(line, "dim"))
   104→    for command in coaching.commands:
   105→        resolved = command.format(
   106→            organize_manual_command=triage_manual_stage_command("organize")
   107→        )
   108→        print(colorize(resolved, "dim"))
   109→    if coaching.footer:
   110→        print()
   111→        for line in coaching.footer:
   112→            print(colorize(line, "dim"))
   113→
   114→
   115→def resolve_workflow_patterns(
   116→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   117→    *,
   118→    synthetic_ids: list[str],
   119→    real_patterns: list[str],
   120→    note: str | None,
   121→) -> WorkflowResolveOutcome:
   122→    """Resolve synthetic workflow IDs and return whether normal resolution should continue."""
   123→    if not synthetic_ids:
   124→        return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="fall_through", remaining_patterns=real_patterns)
   125→
   126→    plan = load_plan()
   127→
   128→    blocked_map = blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   129→    for synthetic_id in synthetic_ids:
   130→        if synthetic_id not in blocked_map:
   131→            continue
   132→        blocked_text = ", ".join(dep.replace("triage::", "") for dep in blocked_map[synthetic_id])
   133→        print(
   134→            colorize(
   135→                f"  Cannot resolve {synthetic_id} — blocked by: {blocked_text}",
   136→                "red",
   137→            )
   138→        )
   139→        print(
   140→            colorize(
   141→                "  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.",
   142→                "dim",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→        if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   146→            return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="blocked", remaining_patterns=[])
   147→
   148→    gated_ids = [synthetic_id for synthetic_id in synthetic_ids if synthetic_id in WORKFLOW_GATE_IDS]
   149→    if gated_ids:
   150→        force = getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)
   151→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   152→        triage_ever_completed = bool(meta.get("last_completed_at"))
   153→        if triage_ever_completed:
   154→            missing: set[str] = set()
   155→        else:
   156→            confirmed_stages = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   157→            required_stages = {"observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "commit"}
   158→            missing = required_stages - confirmed_stages
   159→
   160→        if missing and not force:
   161→            if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   162→                inject_triage_stages(plan)
   163→                meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   164→                plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   165→                save_plan(plan)
   166→
   167→            stage_order = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "commit"]
   168→            next_stage = next((stage for stage in stage_order if stage in missing), "observe")
   169→
   170→            for workflow_id in gated_ids:
   171→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {workflow_id} — triage not complete.", "red"))
   172→            print()
   173→
   174→            _print_stage_runner_guidance(next_stage)
   175→            print(
   176→                colorize(
   177→                    f"  Manual fallback: {triage_manual_stage_command(next_stage)}",
   178→                    "dim",
   179→                )
   180→            )
   181→            print()
   182→
   183→            _print_stage_coaching(next_stage)
   184→
   185→            print()
   186→            print(colorize(f"  Remaining stages: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}", "dim"))
   187→            print(
   188→                colorize(
   189→                    "  To skip triage: --force-resolve --note 'reason for skipping triage'",
   190→                    "dim",
   191→                )
   192→            )
   193→
   194→            append_log_entry(
   195→                plan,
   196→                "workflow_blocked",
   197→                issue_ids=gated_ids,
   198→                actor="user",
   199→                note=note,
   200→                detail={"missing_stages": sorted(missing), "next_stage": next_stage},
   201→            )
   202→            save_plan(plan)
   203→            return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="blocked", remaining_patterns=[])
   204→
   205→        if missing and force:
   206→            if not note or len(note.strip()) < 50:
   207→                print(
   208→                    colorize(
   209→                        "  --force-resolve still requires --note (min 50 chars) explaining "
   210→                        "why you're skipping triage.",
   211→                        "red",
   212→                    )
   213→                )
   214→                return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="blocked", remaining_patterns=[])
   215→            print(colorize("  WARNING: Skipping triage requirement — this is logged.", "yellow"))
   216→            append_log_entry(
   217→                plan,
   218→                "workflow_force_skip",
   219→                issue_ids=gated_ids,
   220→                actor="user",
   221→                note=note,
   222→                detail={"forced": True, "missing_stages": sorted(missing)},
   223→            )
   224→            save_plan(plan)
   225→
   226→    if gated_ids:
   227→        scan_count_at_start = plan.get("scan_count_at_plan_start")
   228→        force = getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)
   229→        if scan_count_at_start is not None:
   230→            resolved_state_path = state_path(args)
   231→            state_data = state_mod.load_state(resolved_state_path)
   232→            current_scan_count = int(state_data.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   233→            scan_ran = current_scan_count > scan_count_at_start
   234→            scan_skipped = plan.get("scan_gate_skipped", False)
   235→
   236→            if not scan_ran and not scan_skipped and not force:
   237→                for workflow_id in gated_ids:
   238→                    print(
   239→                        colorize(
   240→                            f"  Cannot resolve {workflow_id} — no scan has run this cycle.",
   241→                            "red",
   242→                        )
   243→                    )
   244→                print()
   245→                print(
   246→                    colorize(
   247→                        "  You must run a scan before resolving workflow items:",
   248→                        "yellow",
   249→                    )
   250→                )
   251→                print(colorize("    desloppify scan", "dim"))
   252→                print()
   253→                print(
   254→                    colorize(
   255→                        f"  Scans at cycle start: {scan_count_at_start}  "
   256→                        f"Current: {current_scan_count}",
   257→                        "dim",
   258→                    )
   259→                )
   260→                print(
   261→                    colorize(

    13→)
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve
    16→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    17→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    18→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    20→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import (
    21→    load_plan,
    22→    save_plan,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import append_log_entry
    25→
    26→from .override_resolve_helpers import (
    27→    check_cluster_guard,
    28→    split_synthetic_patterns,
    29→)
    30→from .override_resolve_workflow import resolve_workflow_patterns
    31→
    32→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    33→
    34→
    35→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    36→    """Mark issues as fixed and delegate to resolve command UX."""
    37→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    38→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    39→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    40→
    41→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
    42→        if not note:
    43→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
    44→            return
    45→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
    46→        args.attest = attestation
    47→
    48→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = split_synthetic_patterns(patterns)
    49→    if synthetic_ids:
    50→        workflow_outcome = resolve_workflow_patterns(
    51→            args,
    52→            synthetic_ids=synthetic_ids,
    53→            real_patterns=real_patterns,
    54→            note=note,
    55→        )
    56→        if workflow_outcome.status == "blocked":
    57→            return
    58→        if workflow_outcome.status == "handled":
    59→            return
    60→        patterns = workflow_outcome.remaining_patterns
    61→        args.patterns = patterns
    62→
    63→    if not validate_note_length(note):
    64→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
    65→        return
    66→
    67→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    68→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
    69→        return
    70→
    71→    plan: dict | None = None
    72→    try:
    73→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
    74→        state = runtime.state
    75→        plan = load_plan()
    76→        if check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
    77→            return
    78→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    79→        plan = None
    80→
    81→    try:
    82→        if plan is None:
    83→            plan = load_plan()
    84→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    85→        cluster_name = next((pattern for pattern in patterns if pattern in clusters), None)
    86→        append_log_entry(
    87→            plan,
    88→            "done",
    89→            issue_ids=patterns,
    90→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
    91→            actor="user",
    92→            note=note,
    93→        )
    94→        save_plan(plan)
    95→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    96→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
    97→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
    98→
    99→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   100→        status="fixed",
   101→        patterns=patterns,
   102→        note=note,
   103→        attest=attestation,
   104→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   105→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   106→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   107→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   108→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   109→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   110→    )
   111→
   112→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   113→
   114→
   115→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_resolve"]
   116→

    14→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:

    22→

    28→        print(colorize("  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.", "dim"))
    29→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))

    31→

1→"""Policy subcommand handlers for plan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import (
     9→    add_rule,
    10→    load_policy,
    11→    remove_rule,
    12→    render_policy_block,
    13→    save_policy,
    14→)
    15→
    16→
    17→def cmd_policy_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    18→    """Dispatch policy subcommand."""
    19→    action = getattr(args, "policy_action", None)
    20→    if action == "add":
    21→        _cmd_policy_add(args)
    22→    elif action == "remove":
    23→        _cmd_policy_remove(args)
    24→    else:
    25→        _cmd_policy_list(args)
    26→
    27→
    28→def _cmd_policy_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    29→    text = getattr(args, "rule_text", "")
    30→    if not text or not text.strip():
    31→        print(colorize("  Rule text is required.", "red"))
    32→        return
    33→    policy = load_policy()
    34→    idx = add_rule(policy, text.strip())
    35→    save_policy(policy)
    36→    print(colorize(f"  Added rule #{idx}: {text.strip()}", "green"))
    37→
    38→
    39→def _cmd_policy_list(_args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    40→    policy = load_policy()
    41→    rules = policy.get("rules", [])
    42→    if not rules:
    43→        print(colorize("  No project policy rules defined.", "dim"))
    44→        print(colorize('  Add one: desloppify plan policy add "rule text"', "dim"))
    45→        return
    46→    print(colorize("  Project Policy Rules", "bold"))
    47→    print()
    48→    for i, rule in enumerate(rules, 1):
    49→        print(f"  {i}. {rule['text']}")
    50→    print()
    51→    print(colorize(
    52→        f"  {len(rules)} rule(s). These are enforced during triage sense-check and review.",
    53→        "dim",
    54→    ))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _cmd_policy_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    index = getattr(args, "rule_index", None)
    59→    if index is None:
    60→        print(colorize("  --index is required for remove.", "red"))
    61→        return
    62→    policy = load_policy()
    63→    removed = remove_rule(policy, index)
    64→    if removed is None:
    65→        print(colorize(f"  No rule at index {index}.", "red"))
    66→        return
    67→    save_policy(policy)
    68→    print(colorize(f"  Removed rule #{index}: {removed}", "green"))
    69→
    70→
    71→__all__ = ["cmd_policy_dispatch"]
    72→

     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import json
     6→
     7→
     8→class CommandError(Exception):
     9→    """Raised by command helpers to signal a user-facing CLI error.
    10→
    11→    The CLI entrypoint catches this and prints ``message`` to stderr
    12→    before exiting with ``exit_code``.  Helpers should raise this instead
    13→    of calling ``sys.exit()`` directly so that the error path is testable
    14→    and composable.
    15→    """
    16→
    17→    def __init__(self, message: str, *, exit_code: int = 1) -> None:
    18→        self.message = message
    19→        self.exit_code = exit_code
    20→        super().__init__(message)
    21→

    23→class PacketValidationError(CommandError):

     5→lives here; callers import from ``engine.plan_triage``.

    35→)

    37→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38→
    39→
    40→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    41→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    42→    real_modes = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    43→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    44→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    45→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    46→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in real_modes:
    47→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    48→                if ts:
    49→                    return str(ts)
    50→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    51→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    52→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    53→    return None
    54→
    55→
    56→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    57→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    58→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    59→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    60→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    61→
    62→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    63→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    64→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    65→    """
    66→
    67→    def _dimension(finding: dict) -> str:
    68→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    69→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    70→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    71→        return ""
    72→
    73→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    74→    for fid, finding in open_issues.items():
    75→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    76→        if dim:
    77→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    78→
    79→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    80→    for fid, finding in resolved_issues.items():
    81→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    82→        if dim:
    83→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    84→
    85→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    86→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    87→        recurring[dim] = {
    88→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    89→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    90→        }
    91→    return recurring
    92→
    93→
    94→@dataclass
    95→class TriageDeps:
    96→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    97→
    98→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    99→
   100→

    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    38→
    39→def _find_candidates(
    40→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    41→) -> list[str]:
    42→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    43→    candidates: list[str] = []
    44→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    45→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    46→            continue
    47→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    48→            candidates.append(fid)
    49→    return candidates
    50→
    51→
    52→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    53→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    54→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    55→    if issue is None:
    56→        return False
    57→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    58→
    59→
    60→def _supersede_id(
    61→    plan: PlanModel,
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    issue_id: str,
    64→    now: str,
    65→) -> bool:
    66→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    67→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    68→    detector = ""
    69→    file = ""
    70→    summary = ""
    71→    if issue:
    72→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    73→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    74→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    75→
    76→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    77→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    78→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    79→
    80→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    81→        "original_id": issue_id,
    82→        "original_detector": detector,
    83→        "original_file": file,
    84→        "original_summary": summary,
    85→        "status": "superseded",
    86→        "superseded_at": now,
    87→        "remapped_to": None,
    88→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    89→    }
    90→
    91→    # Preserve any existing override note
    92→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    93→    if override_note:
    94→        entry["note"] = override_note
    95→
    96→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    97→
    98→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    99→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   100→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   101→    if issue_id in order:
   102→        order.remove(issue_id)
   103→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   104→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   105→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   106→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   107→        if issue_id in ids:
   108→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   109→
   110→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   111→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   112→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   113→        override["cluster"] = None
   114→        override["updated_at"] = now
   115→
   116→    return True
   117→
   118→
   119→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   120→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   121→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   122→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   123→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   124→
   125→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   126→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   127→        try:
   128→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   129→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   130→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   131→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   132→                to_prune.append(fid)
   133→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   134→            to_prune.append(fid)
   135→
   136→    for fid in to_prune:
   137→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   138→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   139→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   140→
   141→    return to_prune
   142→
   143→
   144→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    state: StateModel,
   147→) -> ReconcileResult:
   148→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   149→
   150→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   151→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    result = ReconcileResult()
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   157→
   158→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   159→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   161→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   162→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   163→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   164→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   165→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   166→
   167→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   168→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   169→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   170→    referenced_ids = {
   171→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   172→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   176→    # clusters that become empty (all issues resolved by scan).
   177→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   178→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   179→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   180→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   181→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    # Check each referenced ID
   185→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   186→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   187→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   188→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   189→                result.changes += 1
   190→
   191→    # Detect manual clusters that just became empty (all issues auto-resolved).
   192→    # Log cluster_done so the plan tracks completion the same way as user resolves.
   193→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   194→        if prev_size == 0:
   195→            continue  # was already empty
   196→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   197→        if cluster is None:
   198→            continue
   199→        if len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) == 0:
   200→            result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   201→            append_log_entry(
   202→                plan,
   203→                "cluster_done",
   204→                issue_ids=[],
   205→                cluster_name=name,
   206→                actor="system",
   207→                detail={"reason": "all issues resolved by scan"},
   208→            )
   209→            result.changes += 1
   210→
   211→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   212→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   213→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   214→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   215→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   216→            continue
   217→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   218→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   219→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   220→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   221→            result.changes += 1
   222→        if not alive_ids:
   223→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   224→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   225→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   226→        result.changes += 1
   227→
   228→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   229→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   230→
   231→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   232→    if resurfaced:
   233→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   234→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   235→
   236→    # Prune old superseded entries
   237→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   238→    result.pruned = pruned
   239→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   240→
   241→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   242→    if result.changes > 0:
   243→        append_log_entry(
   244→            plan,
   245→            "reconcile",
   246→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   247→            actor="system",
   248→            detail={
   249→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   250→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   251→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   252→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   253→            },
   254→        )
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→__all__ = [
   259→    "ReconcileResult",
   260→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   261→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   262→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   263→]
   264→
```

    36→    steps_with_bad_paths: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]] = _steps_with_bad_paths
    37→    steps_without_effort: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_without_effort
    38→    enrich_report_or_error: Callable[[str | None], str | None] = _enrich_report_or_error
    39→    resolve_reusable_report: Callable[[str | None, dict | None], tuple[str | None, bool]] = (
    40→        resolve_reusable_report
    41→    )
    42→    record_enrich_stage: Callable[..., list[str]] = record_enrich_stage
    43→    count_log_activity_since: Callable[[dict, str], dict[str, int]] = count_log_activity_since
    44→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    45→    print_user_message: Callable[[str], None] = print_user_message
    46→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    47→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    48→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    49→    auto_confirm_organize_for_complete: Callable[..., bool] | None = None
    50→
    51→
    52→def run_stage_enrich(
    53→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    54→    *,
    55→    services: TriageServices | None,
    56→    deps: EnrichStageDeps | None = None,
    57→) -> None:
    58→    """Record the ENRICH stage with validation and optional auto-confirm."""
    59→    resolved_deps = deps or EnrichStageDeps()
    60→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    61→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    62→
    63→    resolved_services = services or resolved_deps.default_triage_services()
    64→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    65→
    66→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    67→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    71→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    72→
    73→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
    74→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    75→
    76→    if not resolved_deps.require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    if not stages.get("organize", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
    80→        if attestation:
    81→            auto_confirm_organize_for_complete = resolved_deps.auto_confirm_organize_for_complete
    82→            if auto_confirm_organize_for_complete is None:
    83→                from .validation.core import _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete
    84→
    85→                auto_confirm_organize_for_complete = _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete
    86→            if not auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
    87→                plan=plan,
    88→                stages=stages,
    89→                attestation=attestation,
    90→                save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
    91→            ):
    92→                return
    93→        else:
    94→            print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot enrich: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
    95→            print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
    96→            print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.", "dim"))
    97→            return
    98→
    99→    if not is_reuse:
   100→        organize_ts = stages.get("organize", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   101→        if organize_ts:
   102→            activity = resolved_deps.count_log_activity_since(plan, organize_ts)
   103→            update_ops = activity.get("cluster_update", 0)
   104→            if update_ops == 0:
   105→                if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
   106→                    print(resolved_deps.colorize(
   107→                        "  Note: 0 cluster_update ops logged since organize. "
   108→                        "Proceeding with attestation override.",
   109→                        "yellow",
   110→                    ))
   111→                else:
   112→                    print(resolved_deps.colorize(
   113→                        "  Cannot enrich: no cluster_update operations logged since organize.",
   114→                        "red",
   115→                    ))
   116→                    print(resolved_deps.colorize(
   117→                        "  Enriching steps requires running cluster update commands.\n"
   118→                        '  e.g. desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..."',
   119→                        "dim",
   120→                    ))
   121→                    print(resolved_deps.colorize(
   122→                        '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why no update ops" (40+ chars).',
   123→                        "dim",
   124→                    ))
   125→                    return
   126→
   127→    underspec = resolved_deps.underspecified_steps(plan)
   128→    total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   129→
   130→    if underspec:
   131→        print(
   132→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   133→                f"  Cannot enrich: {total_bare} step(s) across {len(underspec)} cluster(s) lack detail or issue_refs:",
   134→                "red",
   135→            )
   136→        )
   137→        for name, bare, total in underspec:
   138→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   139→        print()
   140→        print(
   141→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   142→                "  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).",
   143→                "dim",
   144→            )
   145→        )
   146→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   147→        print(
   148→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   149→                '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   150→                "dim",
   151→            )
   152→        )
   153→        print(
   154→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   155→                "  You can also still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder, etc.",
   156→                "dim",
   157→            )
   158→        )
   159→        return
   160→
   161→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   162→
   163→    get_project_root = resolved_deps.get_project_root
   164→    if get_project_root is None:
   165→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   166→
   167→    bad_paths = resolved_deps.steps_with_bad_paths(plan, get_project_root())
   168→
   169→    if bad_paths:
   170→        total_bad = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   171→        print(
   172→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   173→                f"  Warning: {total_bad} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk:",
   174→                "yellow",
   175→            )
   176→        )
   177→        for name, step_num, paths in bad_paths[:5]:
   178→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {', '.join(paths[:3])}", "yellow"))
   179→        print(
   180→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   181→                "  Fix paths before confirming enrich (confirmation will block on bad paths).",
   182→                "dim",
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→
   186→    untagged = resolved_deps.steps_without_effort(plan)
   187→    if untagged:
   188→        total_missing = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   189→        print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"  Note: {total_missing} step(s) have no effort tag.", "yellow"))
   190→        print(
   191→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   192→                "  Consider: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --effort small",
   193→                "dim",
   194→            )
   195→        )
   196→
   197→    report = resolved_deps.enrich_report_or_error(report)
   198→    if report is None:
   199→        return
   200→
   201→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   202→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_enrich_stage(
   203→        stages,
   204→        report=report,
   205→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   206→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   207→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   208→    )
   209→
   210→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   211→
   212→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   213→        plan,
   214→        "triage_enrich",
   215→        actor="user",
   216→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   217→    )
   218→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   219→
   220→    print(
   221→        resolved_deps.colorize(
   222→            f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   223→            "green",
   224→        )
   225→    )
   226→    if is_reuse:
   227→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   228→        if cleared:
   229→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   230→    else:
   231→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   232→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   233→
   234→    resolved_deps.print_user_message(
   235→        "Enrich recorded. Before confirming — check the subagent's"
   236→        " work. Could a developer who has never seen this code"
   237→        " execute every step without asking a question? Every step"
   238→        " needs: file path, specific location, specific action."
   239→        " 'Refactor X' fails. 'Extract lines 45-89 into Y' passes."
   240→    )
   241→
   242→
   243→__all__ = ["ColorizeFn", "EnrichStageDeps", "run_stage_enrich"]
   244→

    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class EnrichQualityFailure:
    34→    """Structured enrich-quality validation failure."""
    35→
    36→    code: str
    37→    message: str
    38→
    39→
    40→def run_enrich_quality_checks(
    41→    plan: dict,
    42→    repo_root: Path,
    43→    *,
    44→    phase_label: str,
    45→) -> list[EnrichQualityFailure]:
    46→    """Run enrich-level executor-readiness checks for a phase."""
    47→    sense_suffix = f" after {phase_label}" if phase_label == "sense-check" else ""
    48→    failures: list[EnrichQualityFailure] = []
    49→
    50→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    51→    if underspec:
    52→        total = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
    53→        failures.append(
    54→            EnrichQualityFailure(
    55→                code="underspecified_steps",
    56→                message=f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs{sense_suffix}.",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→
    60→    bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
    61→    if bad_paths:
    62→        total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
    63→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    64→            message = f"{total} file path(s) don't exist on disk{sense_suffix}."
    65→        else:
    66→            message = f"{total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk."
    67→        failures.append(
    68→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="missing_paths", message=message)
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
    72→    if untagged:
    73→        total = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
    74→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    75→            message = f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag{sense_suffix}."
    76→        else:
    77→            message = (
    78→                f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag "
    79→                "(trivial/small/medium/large)."
    80→            )
    81→        failures.append(
    82→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="missing_effort", message=message)
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
    86→    if no_refs:
    87→        total = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
    88→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    89→            message = f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs{sense_suffix}."
    90→        else:
    91→            message = f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs for traceability."
    92→        failures.append(
    93→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="missing_issue_refs", message=message)
    94→        )
    95→
    96→    vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
    97→    if vague:
    98→        if phase_label == "sense-check":
    99→            message = f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail{sense_suffix}."
   100→        else:
   101→            message = (
   102→                f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths). "
   103→                "Executor-ready means: file path + specific instruction."
   104→            )
   105→        failures.append(
   106→            EnrichQualityFailure(code="vague_detail", message=message)
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    # Steps referencing skipped/wontfixed issues
   110→    stale_refs = _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan)
   111→    if stale_refs:
   112→        total_stale = sum(len(refs) for _, _, refs in stale_refs)
   113→        failures.append(
   114→            EnrichQualityFailure(
   115→                code="stale_issue_refs",
   116→                message=(
   117→                    f"{total_stale} step issue_ref(s) point to skipped/wontfixed issues"
   118→                    f"{sense_suffix}. Remove stale refs."
   119→                ),
   120→            )
   121→        )
   122→
   123→    return failures
   124→
   125→
   126→def validate_stage(
   127→    stage: str,
   128→    plan: dict,
   129→    state: dict,
   130→    repo_root: Path,
   131→    *,
   132→    triage_input: TriageInput | None = None,
   133→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   134→    """Check subagent completed stage correctly. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   135→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   136→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   137→
   138→    if stage == "observe":
   139→        if "observe" not in stages:
   140→            return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
   141→        report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   142→        if len(report) < 100:
   143→            return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   144→        # Check citation ratio — must reference specific issues
   145→        cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
   146→        issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
   147→        if issue_count > 0:
   148→            min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
   149→            if len(cited) < min_citations:
   150→                return False, (
   151→                    f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
   152→                    f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
   153→                    f"hashes to prove you read them."
   154→                )
   155→            # Structured evidence validation
   156→            valid_ids: set[str] = set()
   157→            if triage_input:
   158→                valid_ids = set(triage_input.open_issues.keys())
   159→            evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
   160→            ev_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
   161→            blocking = [f for f in ev_failures if f.blocking]
   162→            if blocking:
   163→                return False, blocking[0].message
   164→        return True, ""
   165→
   166→    if stage == "reflect":
   167→        if "reflect" not in stages:
   168→            return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
   169→        report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   170→        if len(report) < 100:
   171→            return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   172→        missing = stages["reflect"].get("missing_issue_ids", [])
   173→        if missing:
   174→            return False, f"Reflect report leaves {len(missing)} issue(s) unaccounted for."
   175→        duplicates = stages["reflect"].get("duplicate_issue_ids", [])
   176→        if duplicates:
   177→            return False, f"Reflect report duplicates {len(duplicates)} issue(s)."
   178→        issue_count = int(stages["reflect"].get("issue_count", 0) or 0)
   179→        cited = stages["reflect"].get("cited_ids", [])
   180→        if issue_count > 0 and len(cited) < issue_count:
   181→            return False, (
   182→                f"Reflect report cites only {len(cited)}/{issue_count} issue(s). "
   183→                "A reflect blueprint must account for every open review issue."
   184→            )
   185→        # Validate skip-reason evidence
   186→        skip_failures = validate_reflect_skip_evidence(report)
   187→        blocking_skips = [f for f in skip_failures if f.blocking]
   188→        if blocking_skips:
   189→            return False, blocking_skips[0].message
   190→        return True, ""
   191→
   192→    if stage == "organize":
   193→        if "organize" not in stages:
   194→            return False, "Organize stage not recorded."
   195→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   196→        if not manual:
   197→            return False, "No manual clusters with issues exist."
   198→        gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   199→        if gaps:
   200→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _ in gaps)
   201→            return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
   202→        unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   203→        if unclustered:
   204→            return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   205→        # Cluster-name mention check
   206→        report = stages["organize"].get("report", "")
   207→        cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(report, manual)
   208→        if cluster_ref_failures:
   209→            blocking_refs = [f for f in cluster_ref_failures if f.blocking]
   210→            if blocking_refs:
   211→                return False, blocking_refs[0].message
   212→        # Cluster operation count check
   213→        reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   214→        if reflect_ts:
   215→            activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
   216→            cluster_ops = sum(
   217→                activity.get(k, 0)
   218→                for k in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
   219→            )
   220→            min_ops = max(3, len(manual))
   221→            if cluster_ops < min_ops:
   222→                return False, (
   223→                    f"Only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged since reflect "
   224→                    f"(need {min_ops}+). Run cluster create/add/update commands."
   225→                )
   226→        # Advisory warnings (non-blocking but informational)
   227→        warnings: list[str] = []
   228→        overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
   229→        if overlaps:
   230→            warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
   231→        scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
   232→        if scattered:
   233→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _ in scattered)
   234→            warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
   235→        high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
   236→        if high_ratio:
   237→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
   238→            warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
   239→        # Orphaned clusters (steps but no issues)
   240→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   241→        orphaned = [
   242→            n for n, c in clusters.items()
   243→            if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
   244→        ]
   245→        if orphaned:
   246→            warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
   247→        if warnings:
   248→            # Return success with advisory message
   249→            return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
   250→        return True, ""
   251→
   252→    if stage == "enrich":
   253→        if "enrich" not in stages:
   254→            return False, "Enrich stage not recorded."
   255→        failures = run_enrich_quality_checks(plan, repo_root, phase_label="enrich")
   256→        if failures:
   257→            return False, failures[0].message
   258→        return True, ""
   259→
   260→    if stage == "sense-check":
   261→        if "sense-check" not in stages:
   262→            return False, "Sense-check stage not recorded."
   263→        report = stages["sense-check"].get("report", "")
   264→        if len(report) < 100:
   265→            return False, f"Sense-check report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   266→        failures = run_enrich_quality_checks(
   267→            plan,
   268→            repo_root,
   269→            phase_label="sense-check",
   270→        )
   271→        if failures:
   272→            return False, failures[0].message
   273→        # Sense-check evidence: file paths + cluster names
   274→        path_failures = validate_report_has_file_paths(report)
   275→        if path_failures:
   276→            blocking_pf = [f for f in path_failures if f.blocking]
   277→            if blocking_pf:
   278→                return False, blocking_pf[0].message
   279→        sc_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   280→        cluster_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(report, sc_clusters)
   281→        if cluster_failures:
   282→            blocking_cf = [f for f in cluster_failures if f.blocking]
   283→            if blocking_cf:
   284→                return False, blocking_cf[0].message
   285→        return True, ""
   286→
   287→    return False, f"Unknown stage: {stage}"
   288→
   289→
   290→def validate_completion(
   291→    plan: dict,
   292→    state: dict,
   293→    repo_root: Path,
   294→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   295→    """Validate plan is ready for triage completion. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   296→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   297→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   298→
   299→    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"):
   300→        if required not in stages:
   301→            return False, f"Stage {required} not recorded."
   302→        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
   303→            return False, f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
   304→
   305→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   306→    if not manual:
   307→        return False, "No manual clusters with issues."
   308→
   309→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   310→    if gaps:
   311→        return False, f"{len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment."
   312→
   313→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   314→    if unclustered:
   315→        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   316→
   317→    # Quality advisories (non-blocking but surfaced to caller)
   318→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   319→    # Check dependency ordering
   320→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   321→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   322→        if name in deps:
   323→            return False, f"Cluster {name} depends on itself."
   324→
   325→    # Check for all-trivial clusters
   326→    all_trivial_clusters = []
   327→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   328→        if c.get("auto") or not c.get("issue_ids"):
   329→            continue
   330→        steps = c.get("action_steps") or []
   331→        if steps and all(
   332→            isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") == "trivial" for s in steps
   333→        ):
   334→            all_trivial_clusters.append(name)
   335→
   336→    if all_trivial_clusters:
   337→        names = ", ".join(sorted(all_trivial_clusters))
   338→        return True, f"Advisory: all action steps are marked trivial in cluster(s): {names}"
   339→
   340→    return True, ""
   341→
   342→
   343→def build_auto_attestation(
   344→    stage: str,
   345→    plan: dict,
   346→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   347→) -> str:
   348→    """Generate valid 80+ char attestation referencing real dimensions/cluster names."""
   349→    if stage == "observe":
   350→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   351→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   352→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   353→        return (
   354→            f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   355→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
   356→            f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
   357→        )
   358→
   359→    if stage == "reflect":
   360→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   361→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   362→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   363→        return (
   364→            f"My strategy accounts for {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   365→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, comparing against "
   366→            f"resolved history and forming priorities for execution."
   367→        )
   368→
   369→    if stage == "organize":
   370→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   371→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   372→        return (
   373→            f"I have organized all review issues into clusters including "
   374→            f"{names_str}, with descriptions, action steps, and clear "
   375→            f"priority ordering based on root cause analysis."
   376→        )
   377→
   378→    if stage == "enrich":
   379→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   380→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   381→        return (
   382→            f"Steps in clusters including {names_str} are executor-ready with "
   383→            f"detail, file paths, issue refs, and effort tags, verified "
   384→            f"against the actual codebase."
   385→        )
   386→
   387→    if stage == "sense-check":
   388→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   389→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   390→        return (
   391→            f"Content and structure verified for clusters including {names_str}. "
   392→            f"All step details are factually accurate, cross-cluster dependencies "
   393→            f"are safe, and enrich-level checks pass."
   394→        )
   395→
   396→    return f"Stage {stage} completed with thorough analysis of all available data and verified against codebase."
   397→
   398→
   399→__all__ = [
   400→    "EnrichQualityFailure",
   401→    "build_auto_attestation",
   402→    "run_enrich_quality_checks",
   403→    "validate_completion",
   404→    "validate_stage",
   405→]
   406→

    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class ReviewPacketContext:
    29→    """Normalized review-packet CLI options shared across entrypoints."""
    30→
    31→    path: Path
    32→    dimensions: list[str] | None
    33→    retrospective: bool
    34→    retrospective_max_issues: int
    35→    retrospective_max_batch_items: int
