```
    37→
    38→def resolve_review_packet_context(args: Any) -> ReviewPacketContext:
    39→    """Parse shared packet options from CLI args."""
    40→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
    41→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
    42→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
    43→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
    44→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
    45→        default=30,
    46→        minimum=1,
    47→    )
    48→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
    49→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
    50→        default=20,
    51→        minimum=1,
    52→    )
    53→    return ReviewPacketContext(
    54→        path=Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    55→        dimensions=dimensions,
    56→        retrospective=retrospective,
    57→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    58→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→def build_holistic_packet(
    63→    *,
    64→    state: StateModel,
    65→    lang: Any,
    66→    config: dict[str, Any],
    67→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
    68→    setup_lang_fn,
    69→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
    70→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
    71→    """Build the canonical holistic review packet payload and lang name."""
    72→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_fn(lang, context.path, config)
    73→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    74→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    75→        state,
    76→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    77→    )
    78→    prepare_fn = prepare_holistic_review_fn or prepare_holistic_review
    79→    packet = prepare_fn(
    80→        context.path,
    81→        lang_run,
    82→        state,
    83→        options=HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    84→            dimensions=context.dimensions,
    85→            files=found_files or None,
    86→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    87→            include_issue_history=context.retrospective,
    88→            issue_history_max_issues=context.retrospective_max_issues,
    89→            issue_history_max_batch_items=context.retrospective_max_batch_items,
    90→        ),
    91→    )
    92→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
    93→    return packet, lang_name
    94→
    95→
    96→def build_run_batches_next_command(context: ReviewPacketContext) -> str:
    97→    """Return the canonical next command for local batch-based review."""
    98→    parts: list[str] = [
    99→        "desloppify",
   100→        "review",
   101→        "--run-batches",
   102→        "--runner",
   103→        "codex",
   104→        "--parallel",
   105→        "--scan-after-import",
   106→    ]
   107→    if context.dimensions:
   108→        parts.extend(["--dimensions", ",".join(context.dimensions)])
   109→    if context.retrospective:
   110→        parts.extend(
   111→            [
   112→                "--retrospective",
   113→                "--retrospective-max-issues",
   114→                str(context.retrospective_max_issues),
   115→                "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   116→                str(context.retrospective_max_batch_items),
   117→            ]
   118→        )
   119→    return " ".join(parts)
   120→
   121→
   122→def build_external_submit_next_command(context: ReviewPacketContext) -> str:
   123→    """Return the canonical next command for external-session submit."""
   124→    parts: list[str] = [
   125→        "desloppify",
   126→        "review",
   127→        "--external-submit",
   128→        "--session-id",
   129→        "<id>",
   130→        "--import",
   131→        "<file>",
   132→    ]
   133→    if context.retrospective:
   134→        parts.extend(
   135→            [
   136→                "--retrospective",
   137→                "--retrospective-max-issues",
   138→                str(context.retrospective_max_issues),
   139→                "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   140→                str(context.retrospective_max_batch_items),
   141→            ]
   142→        )
   143→    return " ".join(parts)
   144→
   145→
   146→def require_non_empty_packet(packet: dict[str, Any], *, path: Path) -> int:
   147→    """Return packet total_files, raising ValueError when no reviewable files exist."""
   148→    total = packet.get("total_files", 0)
   149→    if isinstance(total, bool) or not isinstance(total, int):
   150→        raise ValueError(
   151→            f"invalid review packet shape for path '{path}': total_files must be an integer"
   152→        )
   153→    if total <= 0:
   154→        raise ValueError(f"no files found at path '{path}'. Nothing to review.")
   155→    return total
   156→
   157→
   158→def build_review_packet_payload(
   159→    *,
   160→    state: StateModel,
   161→    lang: Any,
   162→    config: dict[str, Any],
   163→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
   164→    next_command: str,
   165→    setup_lang_fn,
   166→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
   167→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   168→    """Build and validate a holistic review packet without persisting artifacts."""
   169→    packet, _lang_name = build_holistic_packet(
   170→        state=state,
   171→        lang=lang,
   172→        config=config,
   173→        context=context,
   174→        setup_lang_fn=setup_lang_fn,
   175→        prepare_holistic_review_fn=prepare_holistic_review_fn,
   176→    )
   177→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   178→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   179→    require_non_empty_packet(packet, path=context.path)
   180→    return packet
   181→
   182→
   183→def write_review_packet_snapshot(
   184→    packet: dict[str, Any],
   185→    *,
   186→    stamp: str,
   187→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
   188→    review_packet_dir_override: Path | None = None,
   189→    blind_path_override: Path | None = None,
   190→    safe_write_text_fn,
   191→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
   192→    """Persist immutable + blind packet snapshots and return their paths."""
   193→    runtime_root = runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override)
   194→    blind_path = blind_path_override or blind_packet_path(
   195→        project_root_override=runtime_root,
   196→        stamp=stamp,
   197→    )
   198→    packet_dir = review_packet_dir(
   199→        project_root_override=runtime_root,
   200→        review_packet_dir_override=review_packet_dir_override,
   201→    )
   202→    return runner_packets_mod.write_packet_snapshot(
   203→        packet,
   204→        stamp=stamp,
   205→        review_packet_dir=packet_dir,
   206→        blind_path=blind_path,
   207→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   208→    )
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = [
   212→    "ReviewPacketContext",
   213→    "build_external_submit_next_command",
   214→    "build_holistic_packet",
   215→    "build_review_packet_payload",
```

```
    97→def _config_hash_for_packet_contract(config: dict | None) -> str:
    98→    """Return a stable config hash used in prepared-packet reuse checks."""
    99→    redacted = redacted_review_config(config or {})

   101→    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

   104→def _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, *, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, object]:
   105→    """Build normalized invocation contract for prepared packet reuse."""
   106→    requested_dims = sorted(parse_dimensions(args) or set())
   107→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   108→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   109→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   110→        default=30,
   111→        minimum=1,
   112→    )
   113→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   114→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   115→        default=20,
   116→        minimum=1,
   117→    )
   118→    return {
   119→        "path": str(Path(getattr(args, "path", ".")).resolve()),
   120→        "dimensions": requested_dims,
   121→        "retrospective": retrospective,
   122→        "retrospective_max_issues": retrospective_max_issues,
   123→        "retrospective_max_batch_items": retrospective_max_batch_items,
   124→        "config_hash": _config_hash_for_packet_contract(config),
   125→    }
   126→
   127→
   128→def _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   129→    packet: dict[str, object],
   130→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   131→) -> str | None:
   132→    """Return mismatch reason for prepared packet reuse, else ``None``."""
   133→    raw_contract = packet.get(_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY)
   134→    if not isinstance(raw_contract, dict):
   135→        return "missing prepared packet contract metadata"
   136→
   137→    for key in (
   138→        "path",
   139→        "dimensions",
   140→        "retrospective",
   141→        "retrospective_max_issues",
   142→        "retrospective_max_batch_items",
   143→        "config_hash",
   144→    ):
   145→        if raw_contract.get(key) != expected_contract.get(key):
   146→            return f"contract field '{key}' differs"
   147→    return None
   148→
   149→
   150→def _try_load_prepared_packet(
   151→    *,
   152→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   153→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
   154→    """Load prepared packet from query.json when shape and contract match."""
   155→    try:
   156→        qf = query_file_path()
   157→        if not qf.exists():
   158→            return None, None
   159→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
   160→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, RuntimeError):
   161→        return None, "query.json is missing or invalid JSON"
   162→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   163→        return None, "prepared packet payload is not an object"
   164→    if "investigation_batches" not in data:
   165→        return None, "prepared packet is missing investigation batches"
   166→    batches = data["investigation_batches"]
   167→    if not isinstance(batches, list) or not batches:
   168→        return None, "prepared packet has no investigation batches"
   169→
   170→    mismatch_reason = _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   171→        data,
   172→        expected_contract,
   173→    )
   174→    if mismatch_reason is not None:
   175→        return None, mismatch_reason
   176→    return data, None
   177→

   179→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
   180→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
   181→    normalized_results: list[BatchResultPayload] = []
   182→    for result in batch_results:
   183→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
   184→            payload = result.to_dict()
   185→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
   186→                normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, payload))
   187→                continue
   188→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   189→            normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, result))
   190→    return merge_batch_results(
   191→        normalized_results,
   192→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   193→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   194→    )

   221→
   222→    # When no explicit --packet and no explicit --dimensions were given,

   466→
   467→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   468→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   469→
   470→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   471→    """
   472→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   473→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   474→
   475→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   476→    if not summary_path.exists():
   477→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   478→    try:
   479→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   480→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   481→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   482→
   483→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   484→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   485→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   486→
   487→    if not successful:
   488→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   489→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   490→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   491→
   492→    try:
   493→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   494→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   495→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   496→
   497→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   498→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   499→
   500→
   501→def do_import_run(
   502→    run_dir_path: str,
   503→    state: dict,
   504→    lang,
   505→    state_file: str,
   506→    *,
   507→    config: dict | None = None,
   508→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   509→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   510→    scan_path: str = ".",
   511→    dry_run: bool = False,
   512→) -> None:
   513→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   514→
   515→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   516→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   517→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   518→    """
   519→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   520→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   521→
   522→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   523→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   524→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   525→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   526→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   527→
   528→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   529→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   530→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   531→    output_files = {
   532→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   533→        for idx in selected_indexes
   534→    }
   535→
   536→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   537→    if missing:
   538→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   539→
   540→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   541→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   542→        failures=[],
   543→        output_files=output_files,
   544→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   545→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   546→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: normalize_batch_result(
   547→            payload,
   548→            dims,
   549→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   550→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   551→        ),
   552→    )
   553→
   554→    if not batch_results:
   555→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   556→
   557→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   558→    if failures:
   559→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   560→
   561→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   562→
   563→    # Reuse the canonical merge+metadata boundary from normal batch execution.
   564→    raw_batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   565→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   566→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   567→        raw_batches if isinstance(raw_batches, list) else [],
   568→        dimension_prompts=raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else None,
   569→    )
   570→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   571→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   572→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name) if lang_name else []
   573→    merged_path, missing_after_import = _merge_and_write_results(
   574→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   575→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_batch_import_provenance,
   576→        batch_results=batch_results,
   577→        batches=batches,
   578→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   579→        packet=packet,
   580→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   581→        scored_dimensions=scored_dimensions,
   582→        scan_path=scan_path,
   583→        runner=runner,
   584→        prompt_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   585→        stamp=stamp,
   586→        run_dir=run_dir,
   587→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   588→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   589→    )
   590→    _enforce_import_coverage(
   591→        missing_after_import=missing_after_import,
   592→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   593→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   594→        scan_path=scan_path,
   595→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   596→    )
   597→
   598→    # -- import with trusted source --
   599→    _do_import(
   600→        str(merged_path),
   601→        state,
   602→        lang,
   603→        state_file,
   604→        import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   605→            config=config,
   606→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   607→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   608→            trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   609→        ),
   610→        dry_run=dry_run,
   611→    )
   612→
   613→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   614→    if scan_after_import and not dry_run:
   615→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   616→        if lang_name:
   617→            run_followup_scan(
   618→                lang_name=lang_name,
   619→                scan_path=scan_path,
   620→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   621→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   622→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   623→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   624→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   625→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   626→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   627→                ),
   628→            )
   629→
```

```
   287→
   288→def _run_stage_sequence(
   289→    *,
   290→    pipeline_context: PipelineRunContext,
   291→    initial_plan: dict,
   292→) -> StageSequenceResult:
   293→    prior_reports = _load_prior_reports_from_plan(initial_plan)
   294→    stage_results: dict[str, dict] = {}
   295→    last_triage_input: dict | None = None
   296→
   297→    for stage in pipeline_context.stages_to_run:
   298→        plan = pipeline_context.services.load_plan()
   299→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   300→        triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   301→
   302→        if stage in triage_stages and triage_stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   303→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: already confirmed, skipping.", "green"))
   304→            pipeline_context.append_run_log(f"stage-skip stage={stage} reason=already_confirmed")
   305→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "skipped"}
   306→            report = triage_stages[stage].get("report", "")
   307→            if report:
   308→                prior_reports[stage] = report
   309→            continue
   310→
   311→        stage_start = time.monotonic()
   312→        pipeline_context.append_run_log(f"stage-start stage={stage}")
   313→
   314→        si = pipeline_context.services.collect_triage_input(plan, pipeline_context.state)
   315→        last_triage_input = si
   316→        exec_status, exec_result = _execute_stage(
   317→            stage=stage,
   318→            args=pipeline_context.args,
   319→            services=pipeline_context.services,
   320→            plan=plan,
   321→            si=si,
   322→            prior_reports=prior_reports,
   323→            repo_root=pipeline_context.repo_root,
   324→            prompts_dir=pipeline_context.prompts_dir,
   325→            output_dir=pipeline_context.output_dir,
   326→            logs_dir=pipeline_context.logs_dir,
   327→            cli_command=pipeline_context.cli_command,
   328→            stage_start=stage_start,
   329→            timeout_seconds=pipeline_context.timeout_seconds,
   330→            dry_run=pipeline_context.dry_run,
   331→            append_run_log=pipeline_context.append_run_log,
   332→        )
   333→        if exec_status == "dry_run":
   334→            stage_results[stage] = exec_result
   335→            continue
   336→        if exec_status == "failed":
   337→            stage_results[stage] = exec_result
   338→            _fail_stage_and_write_summary(
   339→                pipeline_context=pipeline_context,
   340→                stage_results=stage_results,
   341→                message=f"triage stage failed: {stage}",
   342→            )
   343→
   344→        confirmed, confirm_result, report = _validate_and_confirm_stage(
   345→            stage=stage,
   346→            args=pipeline_context.args,
   347→            services=pipeline_context.services,
   348→            si=si,
   349→            state=pipeline_context.state,
   350→            repo_root=pipeline_context.repo_root,
   351→            stage_start=stage_start,
   352→            append_run_log=pipeline_context.append_run_log,
   353→        )
   354→        stage_results[stage] = confirm_result
   355→        if not confirmed:
   356→            _fail_stage_and_write_summary(
   357→                pipeline_context=pipeline_context,
   358→                stage_results=stage_results,
   359→                message=f"triage stage validation failed: {stage}",
   360→            )
   361→        if report:
   362→            prior_reports[stage] = report
   363→
   364→    return StageSequenceResult(
   365→        stage_results=stage_results,
   366→        prior_reports=prior_reports,
   367→        last_triage_input=last_triage_input,
   368→    )
   369→
   370→
   371→def _finalize_pipeline_run(
   372→    *,
   373→    pipeline_context: PipelineRunContext,
   374→    stage_results: dict[str, dict],
   375→    pipeline_start: float,
   376→    last_triage_input: dict | None,
   377→) -> None:
   378→    if pipeline_context.dry_run:
   379→        print(colorize("\n  [dry-run] All prompts generated. No stages executed.", "cyan"))
   380→        write_triage_run_summary(
   381→            pipeline_context.run_dir,
   382→            pipeline_context.stamp,
   383→            pipeline_context.stages_to_run,
   384→            stage_results,
   385→            pipeline_context.append_run_log,
   386→        )
   387→        return
   388→
   389→    plan = pipeline_context.services.load_plan()
   390→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   391→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   392→    strategy = _build_completion_strategy(stages_data)
   393→
   394→    should_auto_complete = (
   395→        _is_full_stage_run(pipeline_context.stages_to_run)
   396→        and _all_stage_results_successful(
   397→            stages_to_run=pipeline_context.stages_to_run,
   398→            stage_results=stage_results,
   399→        )
   400→    )
   401→    total_elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - pipeline_start)
   402→    if not should_auto_complete:
   403→        _print_not_finalized_message("partial stage run")
   404→        pipeline_context.append_run_log(
   405→            f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s finalized=false reason=partial_stage_run"
   406→        )
   407→        write_triage_run_summary(
   408→            pipeline_context.run_dir,
   409→            pipeline_context.stamp,
   410→            pipeline_context.stages_to_run,
   411→            stage_results,
   412→            pipeline_context.append_run_log,
   413→            finalized=False,
   414→            finalization_reason="partial_stage_run",
   415→        )
   416→        return
   417→
   418→    triage_input = last_triage_input or pipeline_context.services.collect_triage_input(
   419→        plan,
   420→        pipeline_context.state,
   421→    )
   422→    completed = _complete_pipeline(
   423→        args=pipeline_context.args,
   424→        services=pipeline_context.services,
   425→        plan=plan,
   426→        strategy=strategy,
   427→        triage_input=triage_input,
   428→    )
   429→    if not completed:
   430→        _print_not_finalized_message("completion command blocked")
   431→        pipeline_context.append_run_log(
   432→            f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s finalized=false reason=completion_blocked"
   433→        )
   434→        write_triage_run_summary(
   435→            pipeline_context.run_dir,
   436→            pipeline_context.stamp,
   437→            pipeline_context.stages_to_run,
   438→            stage_results,
   439→            pipeline_context.append_run_log,
   440→            finalized=False,
   441→            finalization_reason="completion_blocked",
   442→        )
   443→        return
   444→
   445→    print(colorize(f"\n  Triage pipeline complete ({total_elapsed}s).", "green"))
   446→    pipeline_context.append_run_log(f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s finalized=true")
   447→    write_triage_run_summary(
   448→        pipeline_context.run_dir,
   449→        pipeline_context.stamp,
   450→        pipeline_context.stages_to_run,
   451→        stage_results,
   452→        pipeline_context.append_run_log,
   453→        finalized=True,
   454→    )
   455→
   456→
   457→def run_codex_pipeline(
   458→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   459→    *,
   460→    stages_to_run: list[str],
   461→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   462→) -> None:
   463→    """Run triage stages via Codex subprocesses (automated pipeline)."""
   464→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   465→    timeout_seconds = int(getattr(args, "stage_timeout_seconds", 1800) or 1800)
   466→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   467→
   468→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   469→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   470→    state = runtime.state
   471→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   472→    ensure_triage_started(
   473→        plan,
   474→        resolved_services,
   475→        runner="codex",
   476→        state=state,
   477→        attestation=getattr(args, "attestation", None),
   478→    )
   479→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   480→    if plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_start_blocked"):
   481→        return
   482→
   483→    stamp = run_stamp()
   484→    desloppify_dir = repo_root / ".desloppify"
   485→    run_dir = desloppify_dir / "triage_runs" / stamp
   486→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   487→    output_dir = run_dir / "output"
   488→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   489→    for output_path in (prompts_dir, output_dir, logs_dir):
   490→        output_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   491→
   492→    run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   493→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   494→    cli_helper = _write_desloppify_cli_helper(run_dir)
   495→    append_run_log(
   496→        f"run-start runner=codex stages={','.join(stages_to_run)} "
   497→        f"timeout={timeout_seconds}s dry_run={dry_run}"
   498→    )
   499→
   500→    pipeline_context = PipelineRunContext(
   501→        args=args,
   502→        services=resolved_services,
   503→        state=state,
   504→        stages_to_run=stages_to_run,
   505→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   506→        dry_run=dry_run,
   507→        repo_root=repo_root,
   508→        stamp=stamp,
   509→        run_dir=run_dir,
   510→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   511→        output_dir=output_dir,
   512→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   513→        run_log_path=run_log_path,
   514→        cli_command=str(cli_helper),
   515→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   516→    )
   517→
   518→    print(colorize(f"  Run artifacts: {pipeline_context.run_dir}", "dim"))
   519→    print(colorize(f"  Live run log:  {pipeline_context.run_log_path}", "dim"))
   520→    print(colorize(f"  CLI helper:    {pipeline_context.cli_command}", "dim"))
   521→
   522→    pipeline_start = time.monotonic()
   523→    stage_sequence = _run_stage_sequence(
   524→        pipeline_context=pipeline_context,
   525→        initial_plan=plan,
   526→    )
   527→    _finalize_pipeline_run(
   528→        pipeline_context=pipeline_context,
   529→        stage_results=stage_sequence.stage_results,
   530→        pipeline_start=pipeline_start,
   531→        last_triage_input=stage_sequence.last_triage_input,
   532→    )
   533→
   534→
   535→def write_triage_run_summary(
   536→    run_dir: Path,
   537→    stamp: str,
   538→    stages: list[str],
   539→    stage_results: dict[str, dict],
   540→    append_run_log,
   541→    *,
   542→    finalized: bool | None = None,
   543→    finalization_reason: str | None = None,
   544→) -> None:
   545→    """Write a run_summary.json with per-stage results."""
   546→    summary = {
   547→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   548→        "run_stamp": stamp,
   549→        "runner": "codex",
   550→        "stages_requested": stages,
   551→        "stage_results": stage_results,
   552→        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   553→    }
   554→    if finalized is not None:
   555→        summary["finalized"] = finalized
   556→    if finalization_reason:
   557→        summary["finalization_reason"] = finalization_reason
   558→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   559→    safe_write_text(summary_path, json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   560→    print(colorize(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   561→    append_run_log(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   562→
   563→
   564→__all__ = ["run_codex_pipeline"]
   565→
```

```
    33→def coerce_string_list(raw: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:

    86→def render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
    87→    """Render dimension-specific scan_evidence guidance."""
    88→    return "".join(
    89→        text
    90→        for dim, text in SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION.items()
    91→        if dim in dim_set
    92→    )
    93→
    94→
    95→def render_historical_focus(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
    96→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
    97→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
    98→        return ""
    99→
   100→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
   101→    try:
   102→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
   103→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   104→        selected_count = 0
   105→
   106→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])
   107→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   108→        issues = []
   109→
   110→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
   111→        return ""
   112→
   113→    lines: list[str] = []
   114→    lines.append(
   115→        "Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:"
   116→    )
   117→    lines.append(
   118→        "Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report"
   119→        " issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or"
   120→        " what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out."
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    for entry in issues:
   124→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   125→            continue
   126→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   127→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   128→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
   129→
   130→        line = f"  - [{status}] {summary}"
   131→        if note:
   132→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   133→        lines.append(line)
   134→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   135→
   136→
   137→def _concern_signal_lines(entry: dict[str, object]) -> list[str]:
   138→    """Render one concern signal entry into prompt lines."""
   139→    file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown file)"
   140→    concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   141→    summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   142→    question = str(entry.get("question", "")).strip()
   143→    evidence_raw = entry.get("evidence", [])
   144→    evidence = (
   145→        [str(item).strip() for item in evidence_raw if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
   146→        if isinstance(evidence_raw, list)
   147→        else []
   148→    )
   149→    lines = [f"  - [{concern_type}] {file}"]
   150→    if summary:
   151→        lines.append(f"    summary: {summary}")
   152→    if question:
   153→        lines.append(f"    question: {question}")
   154→    lines.extend(f"    evidence: {snippet}" for snippet in evidence[:2])
   155→    return lines
   156→
   157→
   158→def _iter_valid_concern_signals(
   159→    signals: list[object],
   160→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   161→    """Filter signal entries to mapping payloads only."""
   162→    return [entry for entry in signals if isinstance(entry, dict)]
   163→
   164→
   165→def render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
   166→    """Render mechanically-generated concern hypotheses for this batch."""
   167→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")
   168→    if not isinstance(signals, list) or not signals:
   169→        return ""
   170→
   171→    lines: list[str] = []
   172→    lines.append("Mechanical concern signals — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:")
   173→    lines.append(
   174→        "Confirm or refute each with your own code reading. Report only confirmed defects."
   175→    )
   176→
   177→    valid_signals = _iter_valid_concern_signals(signals)
   178→    capped_signals = valid_signals[:8]
   179→    for entry in capped_signals:
   180→        lines.extend(_concern_signal_lines(entry))
   181→
   182→    extra = max(0, len(valid_signals) - len(capped_signals))
   183→    if extra:
   184→        lines.append(f"  - (+{extra} more concern signals)")
   185→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   186→
   187→
   188→def render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   189→    """Render workflow integrity checks for architecture/integration dimensions."""
   190→    if not dim_set.intersection(
   191→        {
   192→            "cross_module_architecture",
   193→            "high_level_elegance",
   194→            "mid_level_elegance",
   195→            "design_coherence",
   196→            "initialization_coupling",
   197→        }
   198→    ):
   199→        return ""
   200→    return (
   201→        "9j. Workflow integrity checks: when reviewing orchestration/queue/review flows,\n"
   202→        "    explicitly look for loop-prone patterns and blind spots:\n"
   203→        "    - repeated stale/reopen churn without clear exit criteria or gating,\n"
   204→        "    - packet/batch data being generated but dropped before prompt execution,\n"
   205→        "    - ranking/triage logic that can starve target-improving work,\n"
   206→        "    - reruns happening before existing open review work is drained.\n"
   207→        "    If found, propose concrete guardrails and where to implement them.\n"
   208→    )
   209→
   210→
   211→def render_package_org_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   212→    if "package_organization" not in dim_set:
   213→        return ""
   214→    return (
   215→        "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   216→        "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   217→        "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   218→        "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   219→        "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   220→        "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   221→        "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_issues` for directories "
   222→        "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   223→    )
   224→
   225→
   226→def render_abstraction_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   227→    if "abstraction_fitness" not in dim_set:
   228→        return ""
   229→    return (
   230→        "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   231→        "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   232→        "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   233→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   234→        "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   235→        "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   236→        "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   237→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   238→        "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   239→        "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   240→        "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   241→    )
   242→
   243→
   244→def render_dimension_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   245→    return (
   246→        render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   247→        + render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   248→        + render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   249→        + render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   250→    )
   251→
   252→
   253→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   254→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   255→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   256→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   257→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   258→
   259→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   260→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension.  When *dimension_prompts*
   261→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a ``_dimension_prompt`` key with the
   262→    prompt for its single dimension so that downstream renderers can use it
   263→    without extra parameter threading.
   264→    """
   265→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   266→    result: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   267→    for batch in batches:
   268→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   269→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   270→            result.append(batch)
   271→            continue
   272→        for dim in dims:
   273→            exploded: dict[str, object] = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   274→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   275→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   276→                exploded["_dimension_prompt"] = dim_prompt
   277→            result.append(exploded)
   278→    return result
   279→
   280→
   281→def render_dimension_prompts_block(
   282→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],
   283→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   284→) -> str:
   285→    """Render inline dimension guidance so the reviewer sees the full rubric."""
   286→    if not dimensions or not dimension_prompts:
   287→        return ""
   288→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   289→    for dim in dimensions:
   290→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   291→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   292→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   293→            continue
   294→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   295→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   296→        if description:
   297→            lines.append(description)
   298→
   299→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   300→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   301→            lines.append("Look for:")
   302→            for item in look_for:
   303→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   304→
   305→        skip = prompt.get("skip")
   306→        if isinstance(skip, list) and skip:
   307→            lines.append("Skip:")
   308→            for item in skip:
   309→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   310→        lines.append("")
   311→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   312→
   313→
   314→def render_scoring_frame() -> str:
   315→    return (
   316→        "YOUR TASK: Read the code for this batch's dimension. Judge "
   317→        "how well the codebase serves a developer from that perspective. The dimension "
   318→        "rubric above defines what good looks like. "
   319→        "Cite specific observations that explain your judgment.\n\n"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def render_scan_evidence_note() -> str:
   324→    return (
   325→        "Mechanical scan evidence — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\n"
   326→        "The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` with aggregated signals "
   327→        "from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, error hotspots, signal "
   328→        "density index, boundary violations, and systemic patterns. Use these as starting "
   329→        "points for where to look beyond the seed files.\n\n"
   330→    )
   331→
   332→
   333→def render_seed_files_block(context: PromptBatchContext) -> str:
   334→    return f"Seed files (start here):\n{context.seed_files_text}\n\n"
   335→
   336→
   337→def render_task_requirements(*, issues_cap: int, dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   338→    dim_focus = render_dimension_focus(dim_set)
   339→    # Build numbered items; dimension focus items get renumbered dynamically.
   340→    lines = [
   341→        "Task requirements:",
   342→        "1. Read the blind packet's `system_prompt` — it contains scoring rules and calibration.",
   343→        "2. Start from the seed files, then freely explore the repository to build your understanding.",
   344→        "3. Keep issues and scoring scoped to this batch's dimension.",
   345→        "4. Respect scope controls: do not include files/directories marked by `exclude`, `suppress`, or non-production zone overrides.",
   346→        f"5. Return 0-{issues_cap} issues for this batch (empty array allowed).",
   347→    ]
   348→    next_num = 6
   349→    if dim_focus:
   350→        for focus_line in dim_focus.rstrip("\n").split("\n"):
   351→            lines.append(f"{next_num}. {focus_line.lstrip('0123456789abcdefghij. ')}")
   352→            next_num += 1
   353→    lines.append(
   354→        f"{next_num}. Complete `dimension_judgment` for your dimension — all three fields "
   355→        "(strengths, issue_character, score_rationale) are required. Write the judgment BEFORE setting the score."
   356→    )
   357→    next_num += 1
   358→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Do not edit repository files.")
   359→    next_num += 1
   360→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.")
   361→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   362→
   363→
   364→def render_scope_enums() -> str:
   365→    return (
   366→        "Scope enums:\n"
   367→        '- impact_scope: "local" | "module" | "subsystem" | "codebase"\n'
   368→        '- fix_scope: "single_edit" | "multi_file_refactor" | "architectural_change"\n\n'
   369→    )
   370→
   371→
   372→def join_non_empty_sections(*sections: str) -> str:
   373→    return "".join(section for section in sections if section)
   374→
   375→
   376→__all__ = [
   377→    "PromptBatchContext",
   378→    "coerce_string_list",
   379→    "build_batch_context",
   380→    "explode_to_single_dimension",
   381→    "render_dimension_prompts_block",
   382→    "SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION",
   383→    "render_scan_evidence_focus",
   384→    "render_historical_focus",
   385→    "render_mechanical_concern_signals",
   386→    "render_workflow_integrity_focus",
   387→    "render_package_org_focus",
   388→    "render_abstraction_focus",
   389→    "render_dimension_focus",
   390→    "render_scoring_frame",
   391→    "render_scan_evidence_note",
   392→    "render_seed_files_block",
   393→    "render_task_requirements",
   394→    "render_scope_enums",
   395→    "join_non_empty_sections",
   396→]
   397→
```

```
1→"""Canonical public API for tree-sitter specs and phase factories.
     2→
     3→Install with: ``pip install tree-sitter-language-pack``
     4→
     5→Internal layout:
     6→- ``specs``: language-spec catalogs and variants
     7→- ``imports``: import graph + resolver/cache helpers
     8→- ``analysis``: detectors/extractors/complexity helpers
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→from collections.abc import Callable

    16→

    26→except ImportError as exc:

    28→
    29→

    31→    """Return True if tree-sitter-language-pack is installed."""

   126→def get_spec(language: str) -> TreeSitterLangSpec | None:
   127→    """Return tree-sitter spec for a language key, if configured."""
   128→    key = str(language or "").strip().lower()
   129→    if not key:
```

```
   155→class RefreshState(TypedDict, total=False):
   156→    """Metadata for the post-flight refresh pipeline."""
   157→
   158→    postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count: int
   159→
   160→
   161→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   162→    version: Required[int]
   163→    created: Required[str]
   164→    updated: Required[str]
   165→    queue_order: list[str]
   166→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   167→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   168→    active_cluster: str | None
   169→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   170→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   171→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   172→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   173→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   174→    previous_plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   175→    refresh_state: RefreshState
   176→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   177→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   178→    subjective_defer_meta: dict[str, Any]
   179→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   180→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   181→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   182→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   183→
   184→
   185→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   186→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   187→    now = utc_now()
   188→    return {
   189→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   190→        "created": now,
   191→        "updated": now,
   192→        "queue_order": [],
   193→        "deferred": [],
   194→        "skipped": {},
   195→        "active_cluster": None,
   196→        "overrides": {},
   197→        "clusters": {},
   198→        "superseded": {},
   199→        "promoted_ids": [],
   200→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   201→        "refresh_state": {},
   202→        "execution_log": [],
   203→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   204→        "commit_log": [],
   205→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   206→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   212→
   213→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   214→    """
   215→    defaults = empty_plan()
   216→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   217→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   218→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   219→
   220→
   221→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   222→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   223→    return {
   224→        name: cluster
   225→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   226→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   227→    }
   228→
   229→
   230→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   231→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   232→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   233→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   234→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   235→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   236→
   237→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   238→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   239→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   240→    if overlap:
   241→        raise ValueError(
   242→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   243→        )
   244→
   245→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   246→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   247→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   248→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] must be an object")
   249→        if "kind" not in entry:
   250→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] missing required key 'kind'")
   251→        kind = entry["kind"]
   252→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   253→            raise ValueError(
   254→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   255→            )
   256→
   257→
   258→__all__ = [
   259→    "ActionStep",
   260→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   261→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   262→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   263→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   264→    "Cluster",
   265→    "CommitRecord",
   266→    "ItemOverride",
   267→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   268→    "PlanModel",
   269→    "PlanStartScores",
   270→    "RefreshState",

   272→    "SupersededEntry",
   273→    "TriageStagePayload",

   275→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   276→    "empty_plan",
   277→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   278→    "triage_clusters",
   279→    "validate_plan",
   280→]
   281→
```

```
    31→def _validate_dimension_note(
    32→    key: str,
    33→    note_raw: object,
    34→) -> tuple[list[object], str, str, str, str]:
    35→    """Validate a single dimension_notes entry and return parsed fields.
    36→
    37→    Returns (evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_preventing_higher_score).
    38→    Raises ValueError on invalid structure.
    39→    """
    40→    if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
    41→        raise ValueError(
    42→            f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
    43→        )
    44→    evidence = note_raw.get("evidence")
    45→    impact_scope = note_raw.get("impact_scope")
    46→    fix_scope = note_raw.get("fix_scope")
    47→    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or not evidence:
    48→        raise ValueError(
    49→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.evidence must be a non-empty array"
    50→        )
    51→    if not isinstance(impact_scope, str) or not impact_scope.strip():
    52→        raise ValueError(
    53→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.impact_scope must be a non-empty string"
    54→        )
    55→    if not isinstance(fix_scope, str) or not fix_scope.strip():
    56→        raise ValueError(
    57→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.fix_scope must be a non-empty string"
    58→        )
    59→
    60→    confidence_raw = str(note_raw.get("confidence", "medium")).strip().lower()
    61→    confidence = (
    62→        confidence_raw if confidence_raw in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "medium"
    63→    )
    64→    issues_note = str(note_raw.get(DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
    65→    if not issues_note:
    66→        issues_note = str(note_raw.get(LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
    67→    return evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note
    68→
    69→
    70→def _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
    71→    note_raw: dict[str, object],
    72→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
    73→) -> dict[str, float]:
    74→    """Extract and clamp abstraction_fitness sub-axis scores from a note."""
    75→    sub_axes_raw = note_raw.get("sub_axes")
    76→    if sub_axes_raw is not None and not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
    77→        raise ValueError(
    78→            "dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes must be an object"
    79→        )
    80→    if not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
    81→        return {}
    82→
    83→    normalized: dict[str, float] = {}
    84→    for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
    85→        axis_value = sub_axes_raw.get(axis)
    86→        if axis_value is None:
    87→            continue
    88→        if isinstance(axis_value, bool) or not isinstance(axis_value, int | float):
    89→            raise ValueError(
    90→                f"dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes.{axis} "
    91→                "must be numeric"
    92→            )
    93→        normalized[axis] = round(
    94→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(axis_value))),
    95→            1,
    96→        )
    97→    return normalized
    98→
    99→
   100→def _validate_dimension_judgment(
   101→    key: str,
   102→    raw: object,
   103→    *,
   104→    require_complete: bool = False,
   105→    log_fn,
   106→) -> BatchDimensionJudgmentPayload | None:
   107→    """Validate a single dimension_judgment entry. Returns cleaned payload or None."""
   108→    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
   109→        if require_complete:
   110→            raise ValueError(f"dimension_judgment.{key} must be an object")
   111→        log_fn(f"  dimension_judgment.{key}: expected object, skipping")
   112→        return None
   113→
   114→    strengths_raw = raw.get("strengths")
   115→    if not isinstance(strengths_raw, list):
   116→        if require_complete:
   117→            raise ValueError(
   118→                f"dimension_judgment.{key}.strengths must be an array"
   119→            )
   120→        strengths: list[str] = []
   121→    else:
   122→        strengths = [
   123→            str(s).strip()
   124→            for s in strengths_raw[:5]
   125→            if isinstance(s, str) and str(s).strip()
   126→        ]
   127→
   128→    issue_character = ""
   129→    ic_raw = raw.get("issue_character")
   130→    if isinstance(ic_raw, str) and ic_raw.strip():
   131→        issue_character = ic_raw.strip()
   132→    else:
   133→        if require_complete:
   134→            raise ValueError(
   135→                f"dimension_judgment.{key}.issue_character must be a non-empty string"
   136→            )
   137→        log_fn(f"  dimension_judgment.{key}.issue_character: missing or empty")
   138→
   139→    score_rationale = ""
   140→    sr_raw = raw.get("score_rationale")
   141→    if isinstance(sr_raw, str) and sr_raw.strip():
   142→        score_rationale = sr_raw.strip()
   143→        if len(score_rationale) < 50:
   144→            log_fn(
   145→                f"  dimension_judgment.{key}.score_rationale: "
   146→                f"too short ({len(score_rationale)} chars, want ≥50)"
   147→            )
   148→    else:
   149→        if require_complete:
   150→            raise ValueError(
   151→                f"dimension_judgment.{key}.score_rationale must be a non-empty string"
   152→            )
   153→        log_fn(f"  dimension_judgment.{key}.score_rationale: missing or empty")
   154→
   155→    if not issue_character and not score_rationale and not strengths:
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    result: BatchDimensionJudgmentPayload = {}
   159→    if strengths:
   160→        result["strengths"] = strengths
   161→    if issue_character:
   162→        result["issue_character"] = issue_character
   163→    if score_rationale:
   164→        result["score_rationale"] = score_rationale
   165→    return result
   166→
   167→
   168→def _normalize_issues(
   169→    raw_issues: object,
   170→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   171→    *,
   172→    max_batch_issues: int,
   173→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   174→    low_score_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   175→) -> list[NormalizedBatchIssue]:
   176→    """Validate and normalize the issues array from a batch payload."""
   177→    if not isinstance(raw_issues, list):
   178→        raise ValueError("issues must be an array")
   179→
   180→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   181→    errors: list[str] = []
   182→    for idx, item in enumerate(raw_issues):
   183→        issue: ReviewIssuePayload | None
   184→        issue, issue_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
   185→            item,
   186→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
   187→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dims,
   188→            allow_dismissed=False,
   189→        )
   190→        if issue_errors:
   191→            errors.extend(issue_errors)
   192→            continue
   193→        if issue is None:
   194→            raise ValueError(
   195→                "batch issue payload missing after validation succeeded"
   196→            )
   197→
   198→        dim = issue["dimension"]
   199→        note = dimension_notes.get(dim, {})
   200→        impact_scope = str(
   201→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   202→                "impact_scope", note.get("impact_scope", "")
   203→            )
   204→        ).strip()
   205→        fix_scope = str(
   206→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   207→                "fix_scope", note.get("fix_scope", "")
   208→            )
   209→        ).strip()
   210→        if not impact_scope or not fix_scope:
   211→            errors.append(
   212→                f"issues[{idx}] requires impact_scope and fix_scope "
   213→                "(or dimension_notes defaults)"
   214→            )
   215→            continue
   216→        issues.append(
   217→            NormalizedBatchIssue(
   218→                dimension=issue["dimension"],
   219→                identifier=issue["identifier"],
   220→                summary=issue["summary"],
   221→                confidence=issue["confidence"],
   222→                suggestion=issue["suggestion"],
   223→                related_files=list(issue.get("related_files", [])),
   224→                evidence=list(issue.get("evidence", [])),
   225→                impact_scope=impact_scope,
   226→                fix_scope=fix_scope,
   227→                reasoning=str(issue.get("reasoning", "")),
   228→                evidence_lines=list(issue.get("evidence_lines", []))
   229→                if isinstance(issue.get("evidence_lines"), list)
   230→                else None,
   231→            )
   232→        )
   233→    if errors:
   234→        visible = errors[:10]
   235→        remaining = len(errors) - len(visible)
   236→        if remaining > 0:
   237→            visible.append(f"... {remaining} additional issue schema error(s) omitted")
   238→        raise ValueError("; ".join(visible))
   239→    if len(issues) <= max_batch_issues:
   240→        return issues
   241→
   242→    required_dims = set(low_score_dimensions or set())
   243→    if not required_dims:
   244→        return issues[:max_batch_issues]
   245→
   246→    # Preserve at least one issue per low-score dimension before trimming.
   247→    selected: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   248→    selected_indexes: set[int] = set()
   249→    covered: set[str] = set()
   250→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   251→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   252→            break
   253→        dim = issue.dimension.strip()
   254→        if dim not in required_dims or dim in covered:
   255→            continue
   256→        selected.append(issue)
   257→        selected_indexes.add(idx)
   258→        covered.add(dim)
   259→
   260→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   261→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   262→            break
   263→        if idx in selected_indexes:
   264→            continue
   265→        selected.append(issue)
   266→    return selected
   267→
   268→
   269→def _low_score_dimensions(assessments: dict[str, float]) -> set[str]:
   270→    """Return assessed dimensions requiring explicit defect issues."""
   271→    return {
   272→        dim
   273→        for dim, score in assessments.items()
   274→        if score < LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD
   275→    }
   276→
   277→
   278→def _enforce_low_score_issues(
   279→    *,
   280→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   281→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   282→) -> None:
   283→    """Fail closed when low scores do not report explicit issues."""
   284→    required_dims = _low_score_dimensions(assessments)
   285→    if not required_dims:
   286→        return
   287→    issue_dims = {
   288→        issue.dimension.strip() for issue in issues
   289→    }
   290→    missing = sorted(dim for dim in required_dims if dim not in issue_dims)
   291→    if not missing:
   292→        return
   293→    joined = ", ".join(missing)
   294→    raise ValueError(
   295→        "low-score dimensions must include at least one explicit issue: "
   296→        f"{joined} (threshold {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f})"
   297→    )
   298→
   299→
   300→def _compute_batch_quality(
   301→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   302→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   303→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   304→    high_score_missing_issue_note: float,
   305→    expected_dimensions: int,
   306→) -> BatchQualityPayload:
   307→    """Compute quality metrics for a single batch result."""
   308→    return {
   309→        "dimension_coverage": round(
   310→            len(assessments) / max(expected_dimensions, 1),
   311→            3,
   312→        ),
   313→        "evidence_density": round(
   314→            sum(len(note.get("evidence", [])) for note in dimension_notes.values())
   315→            / max(len(issues), 1),
   316→            3,
   317→        ),
   318→        "high_score_missing_issue_note": high_score_missing_issue_note,
   319→    }
   320→
   321→
   322→def normalize_batch_result(
   323→    payload: dict[str, object],
   324→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   325→    *,
   326→    max_batch_issues: int,
   327→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   328→    log_fn=lambda _msg: None,
   329→) -> tuple[
   330→    dict[str, float],
   331→    list[BatchIssuePayload],
   332→    dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   333→    dict[str, BatchDimensionJudgmentPayload],
   334→    BatchQualityPayload,
   335→]:
   336→    """Validate and normalize one batch payload."""
   337→    if "assessments" not in payload:
   338→        raise ValueError("payload missing required key: assessments")
   339→    key_error = normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
   340→        payload,
   341→        missing_issues_error="payload missing required key: issues",
   342→    )
   343→    if key_error is not None:
   344→        raise ValueError(key_error)
   345→
   346→    raw_assessments = payload.get("assessments")
   347→    if not isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):
   348→        raise ValueError("assessments must be an object")
   349→
   350→    raw_dimension_notes = payload.get("dimension_notes", {})
   351→    if not isinstance(raw_dimension_notes, dict):
   352→        raise ValueError("dimension_notes must be an object")
   353→
   354→    assessments: dict[str, float] = {}
   355→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload] = {}
   356→    high_score_missing_issue_note = 0.0
   357→    for key, value in raw_assessments.items():
   358→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key:
   359→            continue
   360→        if key not in allowed_dims:
   361→            continue
   362→        if isinstance(value, bool):
   363→            continue
   364→        if not isinstance(value, int | float):
   365→            continue
   366→        score = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
   367→
   368→        note_raw = raw_dimension_notes.get(key)
   369→        evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note = (
   370→            _validate_dimension_note(key, note_raw)
   371→        )
   372→        if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   373→            raise ValueError(
   374→                f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   375→            )
   376→        if score > HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD and not issues_note:
   377→            high_score_missing_issue_note += 1
   378→
   379→        normalized_sub_axes: dict[str, float] = {}
   380→        if key == "abstraction_fitness":
   381→            normalized_sub_axes = _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   382→                note_raw, abstraction_sub_axes
   383→            )
   384→
   385→        assessments[key] = score
   386→        dimension_notes[key] = {
   387→            "evidence": [str(item).strip() for item in evidence if str(item).strip()],
   388→            "impact_scope": impact_scope.strip(),
   389→            "fix_scope": fix_scope.strip(),
   390→            "confidence": confidence,
   391→            "issues_preventing_higher_score": issues_note,
   392→        }
   393→        if normalized_sub_axes:
   394→            dimension_notes[key]["sub_axes"] = normalized_sub_axes
   395→
   396→    raw_judgment = payload.get("dimension_judgment", {})
   397→    if not isinstance(raw_judgment, dict):
   398→        raise ValueError("dimension_judgment must be an object")
   399→    dimension_judgment: dict[str, BatchDimensionJudgmentPayload] = {}
   400→    for key in assessments:
   401→        if key not in raw_judgment:
   402→            raise ValueError(
   403→                f"dimension_judgment missing entry for assessed dimension: {key}"
   404→            )
   405→        judgment_raw = raw_judgment.get(key)
   406→        validated = _validate_dimension_judgment(
   407→            key,
   408→            judgment_raw,
   409→            require_complete=True,
   410→            log_fn=log_fn,
   411→        )
   412→        if validated is None:
   413→            raise ValueError(
   414→                f"dimension_judgment.{key} must include strengths, issue_character, and score_rationale"
   415→            )
   416→        dimension_judgment[key] = validated
   417→
   418→    issues = _normalize_issues(
   419→        payload.get("issues"),
   420→        dimension_notes,
   421→        max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues,
   422→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   423→        low_score_dimensions=_low_score_dimensions(assessments),
   424→    )
   425→    _enforce_low_score_issues(assessments=assessments, issues=issues)
   426→
   427→    quality = _compute_batch_quality(
   428→        assessments,
   429→        issues,
   430→        dimension_notes,
   431→        high_score_missing_issue_note,
   432→        expected_dimensions=len(allowed_dims),
   433→    )
   434→    return (
   435→        assessments,
   436→        [issue.to_payload() for issue in issues],
   437→        dimension_notes,
   438→        dimension_judgment,
   439→        quality,
   440→    )
   441→
   442→
   443→__all__ = ["normalize_batch_result"]
   444→
```

```
   128→        lang=lang,

   133→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")

   139→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   140→        scan_path=scan_path,
   141→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   145→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   146→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
```

```
    17→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    18→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    19→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    20→
    21→
    22→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    23→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    24→        return ""
    25→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    26→
    27→
    28→def _primary_lang_from_issues(issues: dict) -> str | None:
    29→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    30→    for issue in issues.values():
    31→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    32→            continue
    33→        raw_lang = issue.get("lang")
    34→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    35→            continue
    36→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    37→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    38→    if not counts:
    39→        return None
    40→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    41→
    42→
    43→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    44→    return str(metadata_dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))

    54→    """Compute (score, pass_rate, assessment_score) for a dimension."""
    55→    assessment_score = (
    56→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
    57→        if isinstance(assessment, dict)
    58→        else 0.0
    59→    )
    60→    integrity_penalty = (
    61→        assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
    62→        if isinstance(assessment, dict)
    63→        else None
    64→    )
    65→    reset_pending = bool(
    66→        isinstance(assessment, dict)
    67→        and (
    68→            assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
    69→            or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
    70→            or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
    71→        )
    72→    )
    73→    if reset_pending:
    74→        score = 0.0
    75→        pass_rate = 0.0
    76→    elif integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
    77→        score = 0.0
    78→        pass_rate = 0.0
    79→    elif has_assessment:
    80→        score = assessment_score
    81→        pass_rate = score / 100.0
    82→    else:
    83→        score = 0.0
    84→        pass_rate = 0.0
    85→    return score, pass_rate, assessment_score
```

```
   146→        plan=plan,
   147→        stages=stages,
   148→        stage="sense-check",
   149→        attestation=attestation,
   150→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   155→    *,
   156→    plan: dict,
   157→    meta: dict,
   158→    stages: dict,
   159→) -> bool:
   160→    if "organize" in stages:
   161→        return True
   162→    if "observe" not in stages:
   163→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   164→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   165→        return False
   166→
   167→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   168→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   169→    if gaps:
   170→        print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   171→        for name, missing in gaps:
   172→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   173→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
   174→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   175→    else:
   176→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   177→        if manual:
   178→            print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   179→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   180→        else:
   181→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   182→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   183→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   184→        print(colorize('  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   185→    return False
   186→
   187→
   188→def _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
   189→    *,
   190→    plan: dict,
   191→    stages: dict,
   192→    attestation: str | None,
   193→    save_plan_fn=None,
   194→) -> bool:
   195→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   196→        plan=plan,
   197→        stages=stages,
   198→        stage="organize",
   199→        attestation=attestation,
   200→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→__all__ = [
   205→    "_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete",
   206→    "_auto_confirm_organize_for_complete",
   207→    "_auto_confirm_sense_check_for_complete",
   208→    "_require_enrich_stage_for_complete",
   209→    "_require_organize_stage_for_complete",
   210→    "_require_sense_check_stage_for_complete",
   211→]
   212→
```

```
"""Validation and guardrail helpers for triage stage workflow."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
from collections import Counter

from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
from desloppify.engine.plan_state import save_plan
from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    collect_triage_input,
    detect_recurring_patterns,
    extract_issue_citations,
)
from desloppify.state import utc_now

from .completion_policy import (
    _completion_clusters_valid,
    _completion_strategy_valid,
    _confirm_existing_stages_valid,
    _confirm_note_valid,
    _confirm_strategy_valid,
    _confirmed_text_or_error,
    _note_cites_new_issues_or_error,
    _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm,
    _resolve_completion_strategy,
    _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy,
)
from .completion_stages import (
    _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete,
    _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete,
    _require_enrich_stage_for_complete,
    _require_organize_stage_for_complete,
    _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete,
)
from .enrich_checks import (
    _cluster_file_overlaps,
    _clusters_with_directory_scatter,
    _clusters_with_high_step_ratio,
    _enrich_report_or_error,
    _require_organize_stage_for_enrich,
    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    _steps_referencing_skipped_issues,
    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    _steps_without_effort,
    _underspecified_steps,
)
from ..confirmations.basic import MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN, validate_attestation
from ..helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues, observe_dimension_breakdown
from ..stages.helpers import unclustered_review_issues, unenriched_clusters


def _auto_confirm_stage(
    *,
    plan: dict,
    stage_record: dict,
    stage_name: str,
    stage_label: str,
    attestation: str | None,
    blocked_heading: str,
    confirm_cmd: str,
    inline_hint: str,
    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    save_plan_fn=None,
) -> bool:
    """Shared auto-confirm flow for stage fold-confirm operations."""
    if save_plan_fn is None:
        save_plan_fn = save_plan
    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
        return True
    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
        print(colorize(f"  {blocked_heading}", "red"))
        print(colorize(f"  Run: {confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
        print(colorize(f"  {inline_hint}", "dim"))
        return False

    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
    validation_err = validate_attestation(
        confirmed_text,
        stage_name,
        dimensions=dimensions,
        cluster_names=cluster_names,
    )
    if validation_err:
        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
        return False

    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
    save_plan_fn(plan)
    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
    return True

    *,
```
